// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Backup & Restoration Engine (.ptbackup v1.1)
// =======================================================================

import { PhysioRecordDatabase } from '../db/db';
import {
  CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY,
  extractAndValidateParentIds,
  validateSnapshotReferentialIntegrity,
  type SnapshotEntityRecord,
} from '../db/registry';
import { broadcastVaultEvent } from '../utils/vaultEvents';
import { registerTestHooks } from '../utils/testHooks';
import type { VaultMetaEntity, BackupHeader, EpisodeSequenceEntity } from '../types/clinical';
import {
  subtle,
  deriveBackupKeys,
  wrapDEKForBackup,
  unwrapDEKFromBackup,
  decryptRawDEKForBackup,
  encryptBackupPayload,
  decryptBackupPayload,
  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation,
  validateRawBackupString,
  BackupHeaderValidationError,
  deriveKEK,
  generateVerifier,
  encryptRecordPayload,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  deriveSequenceHmacKey,
  registerSequenceHmacKey,
  computeSessionNumberHmacToken,
  bindDekToGeneration,
  randomBytes,
  CONSTANTS,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES,
  MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK,
  DekExhaustedError,
} from './vault';

export const BACKUP_WARNING_THRESHOLD_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024; // 20 MB (80% of 25MB max limit)

/**
 * Canonical deterministic serialization of the backup header Additional Authenticated Data (AAD).
 * This byte string is passed as the `additionalData` parameter of the AES-256-GCM payload cipher
 * so that any modification to any authenticated field causes decryption to fail immediately.
 *
 * Fields authenticated (in strict order):
 * 1. formatVersion   — Explicit independent format version string ('1.1'), first field for unambiguous versioning.
 * 2. magic           — File type identifier ('PHYSIORECORD_BACKUP_V1.1').
 * 3. exportDateUtc   — ISO 8601 export timestamp.
 * 4. clinicTimezone  — Clinic local timezone (e.g. 'Asia/Baghdad').
 * 5. schemaVersion   — Dexie database schema version number.
 * 6. kdfAlgorithm    — KDF identifier ('PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256').
 * 7. kdfIterations   — PBKDF2 iteration count (600,000).
 * 8. keyExpansion    — Key expansion identifier ('HKDF-SHA256').
 * 9. hkdfHash        — HKDF hash function ('SHA-256').
 * 10. hkdfSalt       — 32 cryptographically random bytes for HKDF domain separation (authenticated as Array).
 * 11. hkdfInfoDekWrap    — HKDF info label for DEK wrapping key ('physiorecord-backup-dek-wrap-v1.1').
 * 12. hkdfInfoPayloadEnc — HKDF info label for payload encryption key ('physiorecord-backup-payload-enc-v1.1').
 * 13. derivedKeyLengthBits — Derived key length in bits (256).
 * 14. backupSalt     — 16 bytes PBKDF2 salt for Backup KEK derivation.
 * 15. backupDekIv    — 12-byte IV used to AES-GCM-wrap the DEK.
 * 16. backupWrappedDek — The raw DEK bytes wrapped under the Backup DEK Wrap Key.
 * 17. dekVersion     — Source DEK version number.
 * 18. encryptionInvocationCount — Recorded aggregate invocation count from source device.
 * 19. maxBackupSizeBytes — Enforced maximum backup size limit.
 * 20. payloadLength  — Length of the plaintext payload (pre-encryption).
 *
 * NOTE: backupDataIv (the AES-GCM cipher IV for the payload ciphertext) is supplied as the
 * cipher `iv` parameter directly — not in the AAD — per AES-GCM specification. The AAD
 * authenticates all security-relevant header metadata orthogonally to the cipher IV.
 */
export const buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD = (untrustedHeader: BackupHeader | Record<string, unknown>): Uint8Array => {
  if (!untrustedHeader || typeof untrustedHeader !== 'object') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('ترويسة النسخة الاحتياطية مفقودة أو غير صالحة لبناء AAD.');
  }
  const header = untrustedHeader as Record<string, unknown>;

  // Strict check: No silent default fallbacks hiding missing or incomplete fields
  const requiredFields = [
    'magic',
    'exportDateUtc',
    'clinicTimezone',
    'schemaVersion',
    'kdfAlgorithm',
    'kdfIterations',
    'keyExpansion',
    'hkdfHash',
    'hkdfSalt',
    'hkdfInfoDekWrap',
    'hkdfInfoPayloadEnc',
    'derivedKeyLengthBits',
    'backupSalt',
    'backupDekIv',
    'backupWrappedDek',
    'dekVersion',
    'encryptionInvocationCount',
    'maxBackupSizeBytes',
    'payloadLength',
  ];

  for (const field of requiredFields) {
    if (header[field] === undefined || header[field] === null) {
      throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(
        `الحقل الإلزامي "${field}" مفقود في ترويسة النسخة الاحتياطية ولا يمكن استخدام قيمة افتراضية.`
      );
    }
  }

  const canonicalObj = {
    formatVersion: '1.1', // Explicit independent format version for canonical AAD
    magic: header.magic,
    exportDateUtc: header.exportDateUtc,
    clinicTimezone: String(header.clinicTimezone),
    schemaVersion: Number(header.schemaVersion),
    kdfAlgorithm: header.kdfAlgorithm,
    kdfIterations: Number(header.kdfIterations),
    keyExpansion: header.keyExpansion,
    hkdfHash: String(header.hkdfHash),
    hkdfSalt: Array.from(header.hkdfSalt as ArrayLike<number>),
    hkdfInfoDekWrap: header.hkdfInfoDekWrap,
    hkdfInfoPayloadEnc: header.hkdfInfoPayloadEnc,
    derivedKeyLengthBits: Number(header.derivedKeyLengthBits),
    backupSalt: Array.from(header.backupSalt as ArrayLike<number>),
    backupDekIv: Array.from(header.backupDekIv as ArrayLike<number>),
    backupWrappedDek: Array.from(header.backupWrappedDek as ArrayLike<number>),
    dekVersion: Number(header.dekVersion),
    encryptionInvocationCount: Number(header.encryptionInvocationCount),
    maxBackupSizeBytes: Number(header.maxBackupSizeBytes),
    payloadLength: Number(header.payloadLength),
  };
  return new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(canonicalObj));
};

export interface ExportedBackupPackage {
  header: {
    magic: string;
    exportDateUtc: string;
    clinicTimezone: string;
    schemaVersion: number;
    kdfAlgorithm: string;
    kdfIterations: number;
    keyExpansion: string;
    hkdfHash: string;
    hkdfSalt: number[];
    hkdfInfoDekWrap: string;
    hkdfInfoPayloadEnc: string;
    derivedKeyLengthBits: number;
    backupSalt: number[];
    backupDekIv: number[];
    backupWrappedDek: number[];
    backupDataIv: number[];
    dekVersion: number;
    encryptionInvocationCount: number;
    maxBackupSizeBytes: number;
    payloadLength: number;
  };
  payloadCiphertext: number[]; // Array of byte values for JSON serialization
}

export interface RestoredBackupSummary {
  patientCount: number;
  episodeCount: number;
  sessionCount: number;
  soapCount: number;
  addendaCount?: number;
  countsByStore: Record<string, number>;
  destinationOperationalDek: CryptoKey;
  restoredInvocationCount: number;
}

export interface BackupPreviewSummary {
  exportDateUtc: string;
  schemaVersion: number;
  clinicTimezone: string;
  restoredPatientCount: number;
  restoredEpisodeCount: number;
  restoredSessionCount: number;
  restoredSoapCount: number;
  restoredAddendaCount: number;
  countsByStore: Record<string, number>;
  currentPatientCount: number;
  currentLatestModificationDate: string | null;
}

/**
 * Inspect and preview incoming backup package against current database state
 * BEFORE any database replacement occurs (Data Loss Prevention & Comparison).
 */
export const inspectBackupPreview = async (
  backupJsonStr: string,
  backupPassword: string,
  targetDatabase: PhysioRecordDatabase
): Promise<BackupPreviewSummary> => {
  // 1. Raw string pre-check BEFORE JSON.parse
  validateRawBackupString(backupJsonStr);

  let pkg: ExportedBackupPackage;
  try {
    pkg = JSON.parse(backupJsonStr);
  } catch {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('ملف النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالح (صيغة JSON معطوبة).');
  }

  if (!pkg || typeof pkg !== 'object') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حزمة النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالحة.');
  }

  const rawPayloadCipher = (pkg as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).payloadCiphertext;
  if (!rawPayloadCipher || (!Array.isArray(rawPayloadCipher) && !(rawPayloadCipher instanceof Uint8Array))) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حقل النص المشفر للحمولة (payloadCiphertext) مفقود أو غير صالح.');
  }
  if (pkg.payloadCiphertext.length < 16) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حجم النص المشفر للحمولة أصغر من وسم المصادقة الأدنى.');
  }

  // Pre-validate untrusted header before deriving any keys
  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation(pkg.header);

  const header = pkg.header;
  const backupSalt = new Uint8Array(header.backupSalt);
  const hkdfSalt = new Uint8Array(header.hkdfSalt);
  const backupDataIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDataIv);
  const payloadCiphertext = new Uint8Array(pkg.payloadCiphertext);

  const { backupPayloadEncKey } = await deriveBackupKeys(
    backupPassword,
    backupSalt,
    hkdfSalt,
    header.kdfIterations
  );

  const headerAad = buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD(header);

  // Authenticated decryption of outer payload (Strict AES-GCM verification)
  const decryptedPayloadJson = await decryptBackupPayload(
    backupPayloadEncKey,
    headerAad,
    backupDataIv,
    payloadCiphertext
  );

  let snapshot: Record<string, unknown[]>;
  try {
    snapshot = JSON.parse(decryptedPayloadJson) as Record<string, unknown[]>;
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشل قراءة حمولة السجلات السريرية في النسخة.');
  }

  // Count records for every registered clinical store
  const countsByStore: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
    countsByStore[storeDef.name] = Array.isArray(snapshot[storeDef.name]) ? snapshot[storeDef.name].length : 0;
  }

  // Query current target database state
  const currentPatients = await targetDatabase.patients.toArray();
  let currentLatestDate: string | null = null;
  for (const cp of currentPatients) {
    if (!currentLatestDate || (cp.updatedAt && cp.updatedAt > currentLatestDate)) {
      currentLatestDate = cp.updatedAt;
    }
  }

  return {
    exportDateUtc: header.exportDateUtc,
    schemaVersion: header.schemaVersion,
    clinicTimezone: header.clinicTimezone,
    restoredPatientCount: countsByStore['patients'] || 0,
    restoredEpisodeCount: countsByStore['episodes'] || 0,
    restoredSessionCount: countsByStore['sessions'] || 0,
    restoredSoapCount: countsByStore['soapNotes'] || 0,
    restoredAddendaCount: countsByStore['clinicalAddenda'] || 0,
    countsByStore,
    currentPatientCount: currentPatients.length,
    currentLatestModificationDate: currentLatestDate,
  };
};

/**
 * Export full encrypted database snapshot into .ptbackup v1.1
 * Note: Local device secrets (vaultMeta) are strictly EXCLUDED from payload.
 * Clinical stores are dynamically gathered from CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY.
 */
export const exportBackupPackage = async (
  database: PhysioRecordDatabase,
  vaultMeta: VaultMetaEntity,
  vaultKek: CryptoKey,
  backupPassword: string,
  clinicTimezone = 'Asia/Baghdad'
): Promise<string> => {
  // 1. Fetch encrypted clinical records for all registered clinical stores
  // vaultMeta is deliberately EXCLUDED as a device-specific store.
  const snapshot: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>[]> = {};
  for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
    const table = database.table<Record<string, unknown>, string>(storeDef.name);
    if (table) {
      const records = await table.toArray();
      snapshot[storeDef.name] = records.map((r) => ({
        ...r,
        iv: Array.from(r.iv as ArrayLike<number>),
        ciphertext: Array.from(r.ciphertext as ArrayLike<number>),
      }));
    }
  }

  const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(snapshot);

  // Enforce memory & payload limits for low-memory devices (25 MB max limit)
  if (payloadJson.length > MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES) {
    throw new Error(
      `حجم حمولة قاعدة البيانات (${payloadJson.length} بايت) يتجاوز الحد الأقصى المسموح به (${MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES} بايت).`
    );
  }

  // Warning when approaching the 25 MB limit (>= 20 MB)
  if (payloadJson.length >= BACKUP_WARNING_THRESHOLD_BYTES) {
    console.warn(
      `[PhysioRecord Security Warning] حجم حمولة النسخة الاحتياطية (${(payloadJson.length / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} MB) يقترب من الحد الأقصى المعتمد (${MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES / (1024 * 1024)} MB). تنبيه: تُضمَّن السجلات المؤرشفة في النسخ الاحتياطية الكاملة ولا يُخفف أرشفتها من حجم الملف. يُوصى بالنظر في حذف السجلات المكتملة نهائياً لتخفيف الحجم.`
    );
  }

  // 2. Derive separate backup keys via PBKDF2 + HKDF with dedicated 32-byte HKDF salt
  const backupSalt = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.SALT_BYTES);
  const hkdfSalt = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.HKDF_SALT_BYTES); // 32 cryptographically random bytes
  const { backupDekWrapKey, backupPayloadEncKey } = await deriveBackupKeys(
    backupPassword,
    backupSalt,
    hkdfSalt,
    CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS
  );

  // 3. Temporarily recover raw DEK bytes using in-memory Vault KEK
  const rawDekBytes = await decryptRawDEKForBackup(
    vaultMeta.wrappedDek,
    vaultMeta.wrappedDekIv,
    vaultKek
  );

  // 4. Wrap raw DEK under dedicated backupDekWrapKey
  const { backupWrappedDek, backupDekIv } = await wrapDEKForBackup(
    rawDekBytes,
    backupDekWrapKey
  );

  // Best-effort zeroing of temporary raw DEK buffer
  rawDekBytes.fill(0);

  // 5. Build Complete Authenticated Header
  const header = {
    magic: 'PHYSIORECORD_BACKUP_V1.1',
    exportDateUtc: new Date().toISOString(),
    clinicTimezone,
    schemaVersion: database.verno,
    kdfAlgorithm: 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256',
    kdfIterations: CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
    keyExpansion: 'HKDF-SHA256',
    hkdfHash: 'SHA-256',
    hkdfSalt: Array.from(hkdfSalt),
    hkdfInfoDekWrap: 'physiorecord-backup-dek-wrap-v1.1',
    hkdfInfoPayloadEnc: 'physiorecord-backup-payload-enc-v1.1',
    derivedKeyLengthBits: 256,
    backupSalt: Array.from(backupSalt),
    backupDekIv: Array.from(backupDekIv),
    backupWrappedDek: Array.from(backupWrappedDek),
    backupDataIv: [] as number[],
    dekVersion: vaultMeta.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
    encryptionInvocationCount: vaultMeta.encryptionInvocationCount || 0,
    maxBackupSizeBytes: MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES,
    payloadLength: payloadJson.length,
  };

  // 6. Canonical Header serialization as AAD
  const headerAad = buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD(header);

  // 7. Encrypt database payload using dedicated backupPayloadEncKey + header AAD
  const { backupDataIv, ciphertext } = await encryptBackupPayload(
    backupPayloadEncKey,
    headerAad,
    payloadJson
  );

  header.backupDataIv = Array.from(backupDataIv);

  const backupPackage: ExportedBackupPackage = {
    header,
    payloadCiphertext: Array.from(ciphertext),
  };

  return JSON.stringify(backupPackage, null, 2);
};

/**
 * 10-Step Safe Restoration Workflow
 * Validates untrusted header -> Derives keys -> Authenticates DEK ->
 * Authenticates Payload -> Validates schema -> Generates new local VaultMeta ->
 * Re-encrypts with isolated in-memory staging counter -> Performs atomic replacement.
 */
export const restoreBackupPackage = async (
  backupJsonStr: string,
  backupPassword: string,
  newDestinationVaultPassword: string,
  targetDatabase: PhysioRecordDatabase
): Promise<RestoredBackupSummary> => {
  // 1. Raw string pre-check BEFORE JSON.parse
  validateRawBackupString(backupJsonStr);

  let pkg: ExportedBackupPackage;
  try {
    pkg = JSON.parse(backupJsonStr);
  } catch {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('ملف النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالح (صيغة JSON معطوبة).');
  }

  if (!pkg || typeof pkg !== 'object') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حزمة النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالحة.');
  }

  const rawPayloadCipher = (pkg as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).payloadCiphertext;
  if (!rawPayloadCipher || (!Array.isArray(rawPayloadCipher) && !(rawPayloadCipher instanceof Uint8Array))) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حقل النص المشفر للحمولة (payloadCiphertext) مفقود أو غير صالح.');
  }
  if (pkg.payloadCiphertext.length < 16) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حجم النص المشفر للحمولة أصغر من وسم المصادقة الأدنى.');
  }

  // STEP 1: Pre-validation of untrusted header structure BEFORE key derivation
  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation(pkg.header);

  const header = pkg.header;
  const backupSalt = new Uint8Array(header.backupSalt);
  const hkdfSalt = new Uint8Array(header.hkdfSalt);
  const backupDekIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDekIv);
  const backupWrappedDek = new Uint8Array(header.backupWrappedDek);
  const backupDataIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDataIv);
  const payloadCiphertext = new Uint8Array(pkg.payloadCiphertext);

  // STEP 2: Derive separate keys using PBKDF2 + HKDF with persisted parameters
  const { backupDekWrapKey, backupPayloadEncKey } = await deriveBackupKeys(
    backupPassword,
    backupSalt,
    hkdfSalt,
    header.kdfIterations
  );

  // STEP 3: Authenticated unwrapping of the DEK (Verifies auth tag on wrapped DEK)
  const rawDek = await unwrapDEKFromBackup(
    backupWrappedDek,
    backupDekIv,
    backupDekWrapKey
  );

  // STEP 4: Authenticated decryption of the payload (Verifies header AAD & payload auth tag)
  const headerAad = buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD(header);

  const decryptedPayloadJson = await decryptBackupPayload(
    backupPayloadEncKey,
    headerAad,
    backupDataIv,
    payloadCiphertext
  );

  // STEP 5: Validate decrypted snapshot records and strict referential integrity
  type SerializedEncryptedRecord = SnapshotEntityRecord & {
    id: string;
    iv: number[] | Uint8Array;
    ciphertext: number[] | Uint8Array;
    recordCryptoVersion?: number;
    dekVersion?: number;
  };
  let snapshot: Record<string, SerializedEncryptedRecord[]>;
  try {
    snapshot = JSON.parse(decryptedPayloadJson) as Record<string, SerializedEncryptedRecord[]>;
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشل قراءة حمولة السجلات السريرية بعد فك التشفير.');
  }

  // Enforce referential integrity: no orphaned episodes, sessions, or notes allowed!
  validateSnapshotReferentialIntegrity(snapshot);

  // STEP 6: Import source DEK as temporary decryptor
  const sourceDek = await subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    rawDek,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['decrypt']
  );

  // Read existing target vaultMeta to determine current and next vaultGeneration epoch
  const existingTargetMeta = await targetDatabase.vaultMeta.get('auth');
  const currentVaultGen = existingTargetMeta?.vaultGeneration ?? 1;
  const nextVaultGeneration = currentVaultGen + 1;

  // STEP 7: Generate a brand new random 256-bit DEK for the destination
  const freshRawDek = randomBytes(32);
  const newVaultSalt = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.SALT_BYTES);
  const destinationVaultKek = await deriveKEK(newDestinationVaultPassword, newVaultSalt);

  const newWrappedDekIv = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.IV_BYTES);
  const wrapAad = new TextEncoder().encode('vault-dek-wrap:v1.1');
  const cipherBuffer = await subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: newWrappedDekIv, additionalData: wrapAad },
    destinationVaultKek,
    freshRawDek
  );

  const destinationOperationalDek = await subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    freshRawDek,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false, // strictly NON-EXTRACTABLE
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );

  // Immediately bind destination operational DEK to its target vaultGeneration epoch
  bindDekToGeneration(destinationOperationalDek, nextVaultGeneration);

  // Derive domain-separated HKDF subkey for atomic session sequence tokens
  const destinationHmacKey = await deriveSequenceHmacKey(freshRawDek);
  registerSequenceHmacKey(destinationOperationalDek, destinationHmacKey);

  const verifier = await generateVerifier(destinationVaultKek);
  const newDekVersion = (header.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION) + 1;

  // STEP 8: Staging In-Memory Counter & Re-encryption across all registered clinical stores
  // The staging counter starts at zero and tracks every AES-GCM invocation under fresh DEK.
  type StagedEncryptedRecord = SnapshotEntityRecord & {
    id: string;
    iv: Uint8Array;
    ciphertext: Uint8Array;
    recordCryptoVersion: number;
    dekVersion: number;
  };
  let stagingInvocationCount = 0;
  const stagedStores: Record<string, StagedEncryptedRecord[]> = {};
  const restoredSessionsByEpisode = new Map<string, number[]>();

  try {
    for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
      const storeName = storeDef.name;
      const records = snapshot[storeName];
      stagedStores[storeName] = [];

      if (Array.isArray(records)) {
        for (const r of records) {
          // Strictly resolve and validate parent IDs from registry
          const parentIds = extractAndValidateParentIds(storeName, r);

          // Decrypt from source DEK (authenticating source dekVersion in AAD)
          const sourceDekVersion = r.dekVersion || header.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;
          const clinicalData = await decryptRecordPayload<Record<string, unknown>>(
            sourceDek,
            storeName,
            r.id,
            parentIds,
            new Uint8Array(r.iv),
            new Uint8Array(r.ciphertext),
            r.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
            sourceDekVersion
          );

          // Track restored session numbers per episode for sequence table reconstruction
          const sessionNum = clinicalData?.sessionNumber;
          if (storeName === 'sessions' && r.episodeId && sessionNum !== undefined) {
            if (!restoredSessionsByEpisode.has(r.episodeId)) {
              restoredSessionsByEpisode.set(r.episodeId, []);
            }
            restoredSessionsByEpisode.get(r.episodeId)!.push(Number(sessionNum));
          }

          // Reserve and increment staging invocation counter BEFORE encryption
          // Enforce MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK (1,000,000)
          if (stagingInvocationCount + 1 > MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK) {
            throw new DekExhaustedError('تجاوزت عمليات إعادة التشفير أثناء الاستعادة سقف استخدام المفتاح (1,000,000 عملية).');
          }
          stagingInvocationCount += 1;

          // Re-encrypt under destination DEK with newDekVersion bound deterministically in canonical AAD
          const { iv: newIv, ciphertext: newCiphertext } = await encryptRecordPayload(
            destinationOperationalDek,
            storeName,
            r.id,
            parentIds,
            clinicalData,
            r.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
            newDekVersion
          );

          // Preserve original entity attributes while attaching new cryptographic wrapper
          const stagedEntity: StagedEncryptedRecord = {
            ...r,
            recordCryptoVersion: r.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
            dekVersion: newDekVersion,
            iv: newIv,
            ciphertext: newCiphertext,
          };

          stagedStores[storeName].push(stagedEntity);
        }
      }
    }

    // STEP 9: In-Memory Pre-Commit Cryptographic Verification
    // Verify that every single re-encrypted entity can be cleanly decrypted with destinationOperationalDek
    for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
      const storeName = storeDef.name;
      const stagedList = stagedStores[storeName] || [];
      for (const sr of stagedList) {
        const parentIds = storeDef.parentKeys
          .map((k) => sr[k])
          .filter((v): v is string => typeof v === 'string' && Boolean(v));
        await decryptRecordPayload(
          destinationOperationalDek,
          storeName,
          sr.id,
          parentIds,
          sr.iv,
          sr.ciphertext,
          sr.recordCryptoVersion,
          sr.dekVersion
        );
      }
    }

    // Pre-calculate reconstructed sequence entities with HMAC tokens for every restored episode
    const reconstructedSequences: EpisodeSequenceEntity[] = [];
    for (const [epId, numbers] of restoredSessionsByEpisode.entries()) {
      const tokens: string[] = [];
      for (const num of numbers) {
        const token = await computeSessionNumberHmacToken(destinationOperationalDek, epId, num);
        tokens.push(token);
      }
      const maxNum = numbers.reduce((max, n) => Math.max(max, n), 0);
      reconstructedSequences.push({
        episodeId: epId,
        lastSequenceNumber: maxNum,
        allocatedTokens: tokens,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      });
    }

    // STEP 10: Atomic transactional activation confined strictly to targetDatabase
    // Architectural Note on IndexedDB Scoping: IndexedDB transactions are strictly scoped
    // to a single database connection. This single read/write transaction clears and commits
    // the pre-validated in-memory staged records entirely within targetDatabase.
    const tablesToLock = [
      ...Object.keys(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)
        .map((name) => targetDatabase.table(name))
        .filter(Boolean),
      targetDatabase.vaultMeta,
      ...(targetDatabase.episodeSequences ? [targetDatabase.episodeSequences] : []),
      ...(targetDatabase.migrationState ? [targetDatabase.migrationState] : []),
    ];

    await targetDatabase.transaction(
      'rw',
      tablesToLock,
      async () => {
        // 1. Clear existing destination data for all registered clinical stores
        for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
          const table = targetDatabase.table(storeDef.name);
          if (table) {
            await table.clear();
          }
        }
        await targetDatabase.vaultMeta.clear();

        // 1b. Clear old local sequence allocations completely (eliminates stale sequence numbers)
        if (targetDatabase.episodeSequences) {
          await targetDatabase.episodeSequences.clear();
        }

        // 1c. Reset half_finalized_soap_repair_v1 in migrationState so restored backups with half-finalized sessions are repaired on unlock
        if (targetDatabase.migrationState) {
          await targetDatabase.migrationState.delete('half_finalized_soap_repair_v1');
        }

        // 2. Insert verified re-encrypted entities
        for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
          const table = targetDatabase.table(storeDef.name);
          const stagedList = stagedStores[storeDef.name];
          if (table && stagedList && stagedList.length > 0) {
            await table.bulkPut(stagedList);
          }
        }

        // 2b. Insert fresh reconstructed sequence allocations matching restored sessions
        if (targetDatabase.episodeSequences && reconstructedSequences.length > 0) {
          await targetDatabase.episodeSequences.bulkPut(reconstructedSequences);
        }

        // 3. Write brand new destination VaultMeta persisting the ACTUAL stagingInvocationCount
        // and advancing vaultGeneration to fence out concurrent stale writes from old vault sessions
        await targetDatabase.vaultMeta.put({
          id: 'auth',
          salt: newVaultSalt,
          wrappedDek: new Uint8Array(cipherBuffer),
          wrappedDekIv: newWrappedDekIv,
          verifierIv: verifier.verifierIv,
          verifierCiphertext: verifier.verifierCiphertext,
          schemaVersion: header.schemaVersion || 1,
          kdfAlgorithm: 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256',
          kdfIterations: CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
          dekVersion: newDekVersion,
          encryptionInvocationCount: stagingInvocationCount, // Persist actual staging consumption!
          vaultGeneration: nextVaultGeneration,
        });
      }
    );

    // Bind destination operational DEK to its newly established vaultGeneration
    bindDekToGeneration(destinationOperationalDek, nextVaultGeneration);

    // Cross-Tab Vault Synchronization:
    // Broadcast vault generation change to inform other open tabs to lock and purge memory
    broadcastVaultEvent({
      type: 'VAULT_GENERATION_CHANGED',
      generation: nextVaultGeneration,
    });
  } finally {
    // Best-effort zeroing of temporary raw key buffers
    rawDek.fill(0);
    freshRawDek.fill(0);
  }

  const countsByStore: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
    countsByStore[storeDef.name] = (stagedStores[storeDef.name] || []).length;
  }

  return {
    patientCount: countsByStore['patients'] || 0,
    episodeCount: countsByStore['episodes'] || 0,
    sessionCount: countsByStore['sessions'] || 0,
    soapCount: countsByStore['soapNotes'] || 0,
    addendaCount: countsByStore['clinicalAddenda'] || 0,
    countsByStore,
    destinationOperationalDek,
    restoredInvocationCount: stagingInvocationCount,
  };
};

// Safely register test hooks for test environments only (isolated/no-op in production)
registerTestHooks({
  restoreBackupPackage,
  exportBackupPackage,
});

FILE: src/crypto/recovery.ts

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Emergency Recovery Architecture (v1.1)
// 
// Dual Recovery Strategy:
// 1. External Encrypted Emergency Backup (.ptbackup outside browser storage)
// 2. Internal Logical Recovery Database (PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB)
// =======================================================================

import Dexie, { type Table } from 'dexie';
import type { PhysioRecordDatabase } from '../db/db';
import type { VaultMetaEntity } from '../types/clinical';
import { CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY } from '../db/registry';
import {
  exportBackupPackage,
  restoreBackupPackage,
  buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD,
  type ExportedBackupPackage,
} from './backup';
import {
  subtle,
  deriveBackupKeys,
  unwrapDEKFromBackup,
  decryptBackupPayload,
  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
} from './vault';

export interface EmergencyRecoverySnapshotEntity {
  id: string; // Primary key: e.g. 'pre_restore_2026-11-06T14-30-00Z'
  createdAt: string; // ISO 8601
  note: string;
  patientCount: number;
  sessionCount: number;
  packageJson: string; // Full encrypted .ptbackup JSON string (NO PLAINTEXT PASSWORD!)
}

/**
 * ARCHITECTURAL CLARIFICATION ON RECOVERY ISOLATION:
 * 
 * 1. Internal Logical Recovery Database (PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB):
 *    - This is a distinct Dexie/IndexedDB database within the SAME browser origin storage environment.
 *    - It provides LOGICAL isolation: application-level clear/bulkPut/restore operations on
 *      `PhysioRecordDB` will not touch or delete records in `PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB`.
 *    - LIMITATIONS: Because it shares the browser origin, it DOES NOT guarantee recovery from:
 *      (a) User or system clearing browser browsing data / site storage.
 *      (b) Origin-wide storage eviction under disk pressure.
 *      (c) Browser profile deletion or corruption.
 *      (d) Device loss or physical hardware storage failure.
 *    - It is therefore categorized as an INTERNAL safety net, NOT an external physical backup.
 * 
 * 2. External Encrypted Emergency Backup (.ptbackup):
 *    - A standalone, fully encrypted .ptbackup artifact saved outside browser storage to the host OS.
 *    - Provides TRUE physical independence from browser profile wipes and site data clearing.
 *    - Protected by PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 (600,000 iter) + HKDF-SHA256 (32-byte salt) + AES-256-GCM.
 *    - The plaintext password is NEVER persisted in IndexedDB, localStorage, or sessionStorage.
 */
export class PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDatabase extends Dexie {
  recoverySnapshots!: Table<EmergencyRecoverySnapshotEntity, string>;

  constructor() {
    super('PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB');
    this.version(1).stores({
      recoverySnapshots: '&id, createdAt',
    });
  }
}

export const recoveryDb = new PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDatabase();

export interface DurableRecoveryResult {
  snapshotId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  patientCount: number;
  sessionCount: number;
  backupPackageStr: string;
}

export interface ExternalSaveResult {
  method: 'fs-access' | 'download-fallback';
  confirmedSaved: boolean;
  fileName: string;
}

/**
 * Creates and persists the pre-restore emergency recovery backup.
 * 
 * Guarantees:
 * 1. Contains 100% of current clinical records.
 * 2. Contains a backup-protected recovery path for the currently active DEK.
 * 3. Pre-validated via in-memory preview before destructive restoration proceeds.
 * 4. Stored in internal logical recovery database (`PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB`).
 * 5. Plaintext password is NEVER stored in IndexedDB, localStorage, or sessionStorage!
 */
export const createDurablePreRestoreRecoveryBackup = async (
  targetDatabase: PhysioRecordDatabase,
  currentVaultMeta: VaultMetaEntity,
  currentVaultKek: CryptoKey,
  recoveryPassword: string,
  note = 'نسخة تعافٍ طارئة قبل استبدال قاعدة البيانات'
): Promise<DurableRecoveryResult> => {
  if (!recoveryPassword || recoveryPassword.length < 6) {
    throw new Error('يجب ألا تقل كلمة مرور حماية نسخة التعافي عن 6 خانات.');
  }

  // 1. Export full encrypted .ptbackup package of current clinical database
  const backupPackageStr = await exportBackupPackage(
    targetDatabase,
    currentVaultMeta,
    currentVaultKek,
    recoveryPassword
  );

  // 2. Mathematically prove 100% full decryptability and integrity of the emergency recovery package
  let pkg: ExportedBackupPackage;
  try {
    pkg = JSON.parse(backupPackageStr) as ExportedBackupPackage;
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشلت معالجة حزمة نسخة التعافي الطارئة: JSON معطوب.');
  }

  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation(pkg.header);

  const header = pkg.header;
  const backupSalt = new Uint8Array(header.backupSalt);
  const hkdfSalt = new Uint8Array(header.hkdfSalt);
  const backupDekIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDekIv);
  const backupWrappedDek = new Uint8Array(header.backupWrappedDek);
  const backupDataIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDataIv);
  const payloadCiphertext = new Uint8Array(pkg.payloadCiphertext);

  const { backupDekWrapKey, backupPayloadEncKey } = await deriveBackupKeys(
    recoveryPassword,
    backupSalt,
    hkdfSalt,
    header.kdfIterations
  );

  // A. Prove unwrapping of the DEK (authenticated tag verified)
  const recoveredRawDek = await unwrapDEKFromBackup(
    backupWrappedDek,
    backupDekIv,
    backupDekWrapKey
  );

  // B. Prove decryption of payload with canonical header AAD
  const headerAad = buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD(header);
  const decryptedPayloadJson = await decryptBackupPayload(
    backupPayloadEncKey,
    headerAad,
    backupDataIv,
    payloadCiphertext
  );

  interface SerializedRecoveryRecord {
    id: string;
    iv: number[];
    ciphertext: number[];
    recordCryptoVersion?: number;
    dekVersion?: number;
    [key: string]: unknown;
  }

  let snapshot: Record<string, SerializedRecoveryRecord[]>;
  try {
    snapshot = JSON.parse(decryptedPayloadJson) as Record<string, SerializedRecoveryRecord[]>;
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشل قراءة حمولة نسخة التعافي الطارئة بعد فك التشفير.');
  }

  // C. Prove that the recovered DEK can cleanly decrypt EVERY record across ALL clinical stores
  const testDek = await subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    recoveredRawDek,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['decrypt']
  );

  // Zero raw DEK copy
  recoveredRawDek.fill(0);

  for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
    const records = snapshot[storeDef.name];
    if (Array.isArray(records)) {
      for (const r of records) {
        const parentIds = storeDef.parentKeys
          .map((k) => r[k])
          .filter((v): v is string => typeof v === 'string' && Boolean(v));
        const sourceDekVersion = r.dekVersion || header.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;
        await decryptRecordPayload(
          testDek,
          storeDef.name,
          r.id,
          parentIds,
          new Uint8Array(r.iv),
          new Uint8Array(r.ciphertext),
          r.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
          sourceDekVersion
        );
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. Persist into the internal logical recovery database
  const snapshotId = `pre_restore_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`;
  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const patientCount = Array.isArray(snapshot.patients) ? snapshot.patients.length : 0;
  const sessionCount = Array.isArray(snapshot.sessions) ? snapshot.sessions.length : 0;

  await recoveryDb.recoverySnapshots.put({
    id: snapshotId,
    createdAt,
    note,
    patientCount,
    sessionCount,
    packageJson: backupPackageStr,
  });

  // Verify write persistence in recovery database
  const confirmed = await recoveryDb.recoverySnapshots.get(snapshotId);
  if (!confirmed || !confirmed.packageJson) {
    throw new Error('فشل حفظ وتأكيد وجود لقطة التعافي في قاعدة بيانات الطوارئ (PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB).');
  }

  // Secondary internal redundancy: cache encrypted package in localStorage (if quota permits)
  try {
    localStorage.setItem('physiorecord_pre_restore_safety_backup', backupPackageStr);
  } catch {
    // Quota exceeded; ignored
  }

  return {
    snapshotId,
    createdAt,
    patientCount,
    sessionCount,
    backupPackageStr,
  };
};

/**
 * Saves external recovery backup file outside the browser origin storage.
 * 
 * Uses File System Access API (window.showSaveFilePicker) when supported for
 * confirmed physical disk write. Falls back to standard browser download otherwise.
 */
export const saveExternalRecoveryBackupFile = async (
  backupPackageStr: string,
  suggestedDateStr: string = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-')
): Promise<ExternalSaveResult> => {
  const fileName = `physiorecord_pre_restore_emergency_${suggestedDateStr}.ptbackup`;

  // 1. Attempt File System Access API (Chromium Desktop native save dialog)
  interface SaveFilePickerWindow extends Window {
    showSaveFilePicker?: (options: {
      suggestedName: string;
      types: Array<{ description: string; accept: Record<string, string[]> }>;
    }) => Promise<{
      createWritable: () => Promise<{
        write: (data: string) => Promise<void>;
        close: () => Promise<void>;
      }>;
    }>;
  }

  const pickerWin = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? (window as SaveFilePickerWindow) : undefined;
  if (pickerWin && typeof pickerWin.showSaveFilePicker === 'function') {
    try {
      const handle = await pickerWin.showSaveFilePicker({
        suggestedName: fileName,
        types: [
          {
            description: 'PhysioRecord Encrypted Backup (.ptbackup)',
            accept: {
              'application/json': ['.ptbackup', '.json'],
            },
          },
        ],
      });

      const writable = await handle.createWritable();
      await writable.write(backupPackageStr);
      await writable.close();

      return {
        method: 'fs-access',
        confirmedSaved: true, // Directly confirmed write to physical filesystem
        fileName,
      };
    } catch (fsErr: unknown) {
      if (fsErr instanceof Error && fsErr.name === 'AbortError') {
        throw new Error('تم إلغاء حفظ ملف التعافي الخارجي من قبل المستخدم.');
      }
      // If other FS error, proceed to fallback
      console.warn('File System Access API failed, switching to download fallback:', fsErr);
    }
  }

  // 2. Download Fallback (<a download>)
  // Note: Browser triggers a download, but we CANNOT confirm whether the user accepted
  // or where it was stored. Thus confirmedSaved is false.
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
    const blob = new Blob([backupPackageStr], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = fileName;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  return {
    method: 'download-fallback',
    confirmedSaved: false, // Dispatched, but physical disk persistence cannot be verified by JS
    fileName,
  };
};

/**
 * Restore an emergency recovery snapshot from internal logical recovery database
 */
export const restoreFromEmergencySnapshot = async (
  snapshotId: string,
  recoveryPassword: string,
  newVaultPassword: string,
  targetDatabase: PhysioRecordDatabase
) => {
  const snapshot = await recoveryDb.recoverySnapshots.get(snapshotId);
  if (!snapshot) {
    throw new Error(`لم يتم العثور على لقطة التعافي بالمعرف: ${snapshotId}`);
  }

  return await restoreBackupPackage(
    snapshot.packageJson,
    recoveryPassword,
    newVaultPassword,
    targetDatabase
  );
};

FILE: src/crypto/vault.ts

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Cryptographic Engine (Web Crypto API — Envelope Encryption v1.1)
// 
// Derived and adapted in part from Niema-Lab/PhysioMeter (src/crypto.js)
// Licensed under the GNU General Public License v3.0 (GPL-3.0).
// Original Copyright (c) 2024-2025 Niema-Lab.
// =======================================================================

/**
 * CRYPTOGRAPHIC POLICY SPECIFICATION & ACCURACY NOTICE:
 * 
 * 1. Application-Managed Non-Extractable CryptoKey:
 *    - Setting `extractable: false` creates an application-managed non-extractable
 *      CryptoKey inside the Web Crypto API subsystem.
 *    - NOTICE: This does NOT imply guaranteed hardware-backed storage (such as a TPM,
 *      Secure Enclave, or HSM), nor does it provide hardware-level protection.
 *    - Non-extractability prevents programmatic API-based key export via `subtle.exportKey('raw')`.
 *    - It DOES NOT protect against malicious JavaScript code executing within the application's
 *      trusted origin (e.g. XSS attacks or developer-tools memory inspection).
 *    - Best-effort zeroing (`rawDek.fill(0)`): Overwriting typed arrays is a best-effort
 *      application-level hygiene practice in the JavaScript garbage-collected runtime; it does
 *      not guarantee physical silicon-level memory erasure.
 * 
 * 2. Complete HKDF Parameters & Domain Separation:
 *    - Hash Function: SHA-256.
 *    - HKDF Salt: Dedicated cryptographically secure 32-byte random salt (`hkdfSalt`).
 *    - Derived Key Length: 256 bits (AES-256-GCM).
 *    - Info Strings:
 *      (a) DEK Wrapping: 'physiorecord-backup-dek-wrap-v1.1'
 *      (b) Payload Encryption: 'physiorecord-backup-payload-enc-v1.1'
 *    - Keys are NEVER reused across cryptographic purposes.
 *    - Independent 96-bit IVs and distinct AAD domains are enforced.
 * 
 * 3. IV Nonce Accounting & Key-Usage Limit:
 *    - Every AES-GCM operation uses a fresh, random 96-bit (12-byte) IV from CSPRNG.
 *    - The invocation counter (`encryptionInvocationCount`) serves as an aggregate key-usage limit
 *      (tracking progress toward rotation threshold), NOT as a sequential IV or a mathematical
 *      guarantee of random IV uniqueness.
 *    - Atomic Reservation: The counter is reserved and committed transactionally BEFORE encryption.
 *      If encryption or persistence fails, the increment is retained rather than decremented.
 *    - Fresh DEK on Restore: Every backup restoration generates a fresh random DEK and re-encrypts
 *      all records with a new `dekVersion`, resetting the new DEK's usage counter to 0.
 *    - Rotation Threshold: 1,000,000 invocations (conservative NIST SP 800-38D bound).
 *    - Single-Active-Device Workflow: A single active clinic device at a time is supported.
 * 
 * 4. Maximum Backup Size Limit (Android Low-Memory Safety):
 *    - Maximum tested backup payload limit: 25 MB (`MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024`).
 *    - This safely accommodates ~60,000 to 100,000 clinical records while avoiding tab crashes
 *      on memory-constrained mobile devices.
 */

import {
  VaultGenerationChangedError,
  UnboundVaultKeyError,
  type VaultMetaEntity,
} from '../types/clinical';
import { registerTestHooks } from '../utils/testHooks';

const DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS = 600_000;
const SALT_BYTES = 16;
export const HKDF_SALT_BYTES = 32;
const IV_BYTES = 12;
const VERIFIER_PLAINTEXT = 'PHYSIORECORD_VAULT_OK';
export const CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION = 1;
export const CURRENT_DEK_VERSION = 1;
export const MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK = 1_000_000;
export const CONSERVATIVE_ROTATION_THRESHOLD = MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK;
export const MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024; // 25 MB

export type CryptoBuffer = Uint8Array | ArrayBuffer;

export interface SubtleCryptoCompat {
  importKey(
    format: 'raw',
    keyData: CryptoBuffer,
    algorithm: string | Record<string, unknown>,
    extractable: boolean,
    keyUsages: KeyUsage[]
  ): Promise<CryptoKey>;
  deriveKey(
    algorithm: Record<string, unknown>,
    baseKey: CryptoKey,
    derivedKeyType: Record<string, unknown>,
    extractable: boolean,
    keyUsages: KeyUsage[]
  ): Promise<CryptoKey>;
  deriveBits(
    algorithm: Record<string, unknown>,
    baseKey: CryptoKey,
    length: number
  ): Promise<ArrayBuffer>;
  encrypt(
    algorithm: Record<string, unknown>,
    key: CryptoKey,
    data: CryptoBuffer
  ): Promise<ArrayBuffer>;
  decrypt(
    algorithm: Record<string, unknown>,
    key: CryptoKey,
    data: CryptoBuffer
  ): Promise<ArrayBuffer>;
  sign(
    algorithm: string | Record<string, unknown>,
    key: CryptoKey,
    data: CryptoBuffer
  ): Promise<ArrayBuffer>;
  digest(
    algorithm: string,
    data: CryptoBuffer
  ): Promise<ArrayBuffer>;
  exportKey(
    format: 'raw',
    key: CryptoKey
  ): Promise<ArrayBuffer>;
  generateKey(
    algorithm: Record<string, unknown>,
    extractable: boolean,
    keyUsages: KeyUsage[]
  ): Promise<CryptoKey>;
}

export const subtle: SubtleCryptoCompat = crypto.subtle as unknown as SubtleCryptoCompat;

export interface VaultDatabaseHandle {
  vaultMeta: {
    get(id: string): Promise<VaultMetaEntity | undefined>;
    update(id: string, changes: Partial<VaultMetaEntity>): Promise<number>;
  };
  transaction<T>(mode: 'rw', table: unknown, scope: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
}

export class LockedVaultError extends Error {
  constructor(message = 'قاعدة البيانات مقفلة. يرجى إدخال كلمة المرور أولاً.') {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'LockedVaultError';
  }
}

export class DecryptionAuthError extends Error {
  constructor(message = 'فشل التحقق من مصادقة السجل. قد تكون البيانات معطوبة أو تم التلاعب بها.') {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'DecryptionAuthError';
  }
}

export class DekExhaustedError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message = 'تم الوصول إلى الحد الأقصى لاستخدام مفتاح التشفير (1,000,000 عملية). يلزم تدوير مفتاح DEK بشكل آمن قبل المتابعة.'
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'DekExhaustedError';
  }
}

export class BackupHeaderValidationError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'BackupHeaderValidationError';
  }
}

/**
 * Generate cryptographically secure random bytes
 */
export const randomBytes = (n: number): Uint8Array => {
  const buf = new Uint8Array(n);
  crypto.getRandomValues(buf);
  return buf;
};

/**
 * Derive Key Encryption Key (KEK) from password and salt
 */
export const deriveKEK = async (
  password: string,
  salt: Uint8Array,
  iterations = DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS
): Promise<CryptoKey> => {
  const baseKey = await subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    new TextEncoder().encode(password),
    'PBKDF2',
    false,
    ['deriveKey']
  );

  return subtle.deriveKey(
    {
      name: 'PBKDF2',
      salt,
      iterations,
      hash: 'SHA-256',
    },
    baseKey,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Operational DEK (Non-Extractable)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------

export interface InitializedVaultKeypair {
  operationalDek: CryptoKey; // extractable: false
  wrappedDek: Uint8Array;
  wrappedDekIv: Uint8Array;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Domain-Separated HMAC Key Derivation for Session Sequence Tokens (Phase 2B.2)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------

const sequenceHmacKeyRegistry = new WeakMap<CryptoKey, CryptoKey>();

export const registerSequenceHmacKey = (dek: CryptoKey, hmacKey: CryptoKey): void => {
  sequenceHmacKeyRegistry.set(dek, hmacKey);
};

export const getSequenceHmacKey = (dek: CryptoKey): CryptoKey | undefined => {
  return sequenceHmacKeyRegistry.get(dek);
};

/**
 * Derive an independent HMAC-SHA-256 subkey from master DEK bytes using HKDF-SHA-256.
 * Uses dedicated domain-separated salt and info parameters to ensure cryptographic isolation.
 */
export const deriveSequenceHmacKey = async (rawDekBytes: Uint8Array): Promise<CryptoKey> => {
  const hkdfKey = await subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    rawDekBytes,
    'HKDF',
    false,
    ['deriveKey']
  );

  return await subtle.deriveKey(
    {
      name: 'HKDF',
      hash: 'SHA-256',
      salt: new TextEncoder().encode('physiorecord-sequence-hkdf-salt-v1.3'),
      info: new TextEncoder().encode('physiorecord-session-sequence-token-v1.3'),
    },
    hkdfKey,
    {
      name: 'HMAC',
      hash: 'SHA-256',
      length: 256,
    },
    false, // strictly non-extractable!
    ['sign', 'verify']
  );
};

/**
 * Compute an opaque, non-reversible HMAC-SHA-256 token for a session number within an episode.
 * Protects session numbers from being stored as plaintext in IndexedDB sequence tables.
 * Canonical input: `physiorecord:seq:${episodeId}:${Number(sessionNumber)}`
 */
export const computeSessionNumberHmacToken = async (
  dek: CryptoKey,
  episodeId: string,
  sessionNumber: number
): Promise<string> => {
  const normalizedNum = Number(sessionNumber);
  const canonicalInput = `physiorecord:seq:${episodeId}:${normalizedNum}`;
  const dataBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalInput);

  let hmacKey = getSequenceHmacKey(dek);

  if (!hmacKey) {
    // Fallback: If hmacKey is not registered, use SHA-256 digest of canonical input as a deterministic hash
    const digestBuffer = await subtle.digest('SHA-256', dataBytes);
    return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digestBuffer))
      .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
      .join('');
  }

  const sigBuffer = await subtle.sign('HMAC', hmacKey, dataBytes);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(sigBuffer))
    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('');
};

// Map binding each in-memory operational CryptoKey (DEK) to its loaded vault generation epoch
const dekGenerationMap = new WeakMap<CryptoKey, number>();

/**
 * Bind an operational CryptoKey (DEK) to its loaded vault generation epoch.
 */
export function bindDekToGeneration(dek: CryptoKey, generation: number): void {
  if (dek && typeof generation === 'number') {
    dekGenerationMap.set(dek, generation);
  }
}

/**
 * Retrieve the vault generation epoch bound to an operational DEK.
 */
export function getDekGeneration(dek: CryptoKey): number | undefined {
  if (!dek) return undefined;
  return dekGenerationMap.get(dek);
}

/**
 * Generate a new random 256-bit DEK, wrap it under Vault KEK,
 * and import the operational key as NON-EXTRACTABLE.
 */
export const generateAndWrapOperationalDEK = async (
  vaultKek: CryptoKey,
  generation = 1
): Promise<InitializedVaultKeypair> => {
  const rawDek = randomBytes(32); // 256 bits

  // 1. Wrap raw DEK under Vault KEK using AES-256-GCM with domain AAD
  const wrappedDekIv = randomBytes(IV_BYTES);
  const wrapAad = new TextEncoder().encode('vault-dek-wrap:v1.1');
  const cipherBuffer = await subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: wrappedDekIv, additionalData: wrapAad },
    vaultKek,
    rawDek
  );

  // 2. Import operational DEK as strictly NON-EXTRACTABLE
  const operationalDek = await subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    rawDek,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false, // extractable: false (prevent API export)
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );

  // 3. Bind DEK to vault generation epoch
  bindDekToGeneration(operationalDek, generation);

  // 4. Derive domain-separated HKDF subkey for atomic session sequence tokens
  const hmacKey = await deriveSequenceHmacKey(rawDek);
  registerSequenceHmacKey(operationalDek, hmacKey);

  // 5. Best-effort zeroing of transient raw bytes in JS runtime
  rawDek.fill(0);

  return {
    operationalDek,
    wrappedDek: new Uint8Array(cipherBuffer),
    wrappedDekIv,
  };
};

/**
 * Unwrap the stored DEK using Vault KEK and import as NON-EXTRACTABLE.
 */
export const unwrapOperationalDEK = async (
  wrappedDek: Uint8Array,
  wrappedDekIv: Uint8Array,
  vaultKek: CryptoKey,
  generation: number = 1
): Promise<CryptoKey> => {
  try {
    const wrapAad = new TextEncoder().encode('vault-dek-wrap:v1.1');
    const rawDekBuffer = await subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: wrappedDekIv, additionalData: wrapAad },
      vaultKek,
      wrappedDek
    );

    const rawDekBytes = new Uint8Array(rawDekBuffer);
    const operationalDek = await subtle.importKey(
      'raw',
      rawDekBytes,
      { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
      false, // strictly NON-EXTRACTABLE!
      ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
    );

    // Bind DEK to vault generation epoch
    bindDekToGeneration(operationalDek, generation);

    // Derive domain-separated HKDF subkey for atomic session sequence tokens
    const hmacKey = await deriveSequenceHmacKey(rawDekBytes);
    registerSequenceHmacKey(operationalDek, hmacKey);

    // Best-effort cleanup of raw bytes
    rawDekBytes.fill(0);

    return operationalDek;
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشل فك تغليف مفتاح البيانات DEK. كلمة المرور غير صحيحة أو تم تعديل المفتاح المغلف.');
  }
};

/**
 * Backward compatibility helper for wrapping raw DEK
 */
export const wrapDEK = async (
  rawDekOrKey: Uint8Array | CryptoKey,
  kek: CryptoKey
): Promise<{ wrappedDek: Uint8Array; wrappedDekIv: Uint8Array }> => {
  let rawBytes: Uint8Array;
  if (rawDekOrKey instanceof Uint8Array) {
    rawBytes = rawDekOrKey;
  } else {
    rawBytes = new Uint8Array(await subtle.exportKey('raw', rawDekOrKey));
  }
  const iv = randomBytes(IV_BYTES);
  const wrapAad = new TextEncoder().encode('vault-dek-wrap:v1.1');
  const ciphertext = await subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: wrapAad },
    kek,
    rawBytes
  );
  return {
    wrappedDek: new Uint8Array(ciphertext),
    wrappedDekIv: iv,
  };
};

/**
 * Backward compatibility helper for unwrapping DEK
 */
export const unwrapDEK = async (
  wrappedDek: Uint8Array,
  wrappedDekIv: Uint8Array,
  kek: CryptoKey
): Promise<CryptoKey> => {
  return unwrapOperationalDEK(wrappedDek, wrappedDekIv, kek);
};

/**
 * Generate a standalone non-extractable DEK (for testing purposes)
 */
export const generateDEK = async (extractable = false, generation = 1): Promise<CryptoKey> => {
  const key = await subtle.generateKey(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    extractable,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );
  bindDekToGeneration(key, generation);
  return key;
};

/**
 * Temporarily recover raw DEK bytes using in-memory Vault KEK for backup export.
 * Note: The operational DEK remains non-extractable and untouched.
 */
export const decryptRawDEKForBackup = async (
  wrappedDek: Uint8Array,
  wrappedDekIv: Uint8Array,
  vaultKek: CryptoKey
): Promise<Uint8Array> => {
  try {
    const wrapAad = new TextEncoder().encode('vault-dek-wrap:v1.1');
    const rawDekBuffer = await subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: wrappedDekIv, additionalData: wrapAad },
      vaultKek,
      wrappedDek
    );
    return new Uint8Array(rawDekBuffer);
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشل استخراج مفتاح البيانات لإجراء النسخ الاحتياطي.');
  }
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Complete HKDF Key Separation
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------

export interface BackupKeys {
  backupDekWrapKey: CryptoKey;
  backupPayloadEncKey: CryptoKey;
}

/**
 * Derive separate, domain-isolated keys for backup operations using HKDF-SHA256:
 * 1. backupDekWrapKey: For wrapping the DEK
 * 2. backupPayloadEncKey: For encrypting the database payload snapshot
 */
export const deriveBackupKeys = async (
  password: string,
  pbkdf2Salt: Uint8Array,
  hkdfSalt: Uint8Array,
  iterations = DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS
): Promise<BackupKeys> => {
  // Step 1: PBKDF2 stretching to derive 256-bit master key material
  const baseKey = await subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    new TextEncoder().encode(password),
    'PBKDF2',
    false,
    ['deriveBits']
  );

  const masterBits = await subtle.deriveBits(
    {
      name: 'PBKDF2',
      salt: pbkdf2Salt,
      iterations,
      hash: 'SHA-256',
    },
    baseKey,
    256 // 32 bytes
  );

  // Step 2: Import master bits as HKDF base key
  const hkdfKey = await subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    masterBits,
    'HKDF',
    false,
    ['deriveKey']
  );

  // Clear transient master bits
  new Uint8Array(masterBits).fill(0);

  // Step 3: Domain-separated Key 1 for wrapping DEK
  const backupDekWrapKey = await subtle.deriveKey(
    {
      name: 'HKDF',
      hash: 'SHA-256',
      salt: hkdfSalt, // Dedicated random HKDF salt
      info: new TextEncoder().encode('physiorecord-backup-dek-wrap-v1.1'),
    },
    hkdfKey,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );

  // Step 4: Domain-separated Key 2 for encrypting database payload
  const backupPayloadEncKey = await subtle.deriveKey(
    {
      name: 'HKDF',
      hash: 'SHA-256',
      salt: hkdfSalt, // Dedicated random HKDF salt
      info: new TextEncoder().encode('physiorecord-backup-payload-enc-v1.1'),
    },
    hkdfKey,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );

  return { backupDekWrapKey, backupPayloadEncKey };
};

/**
 * Wrap raw DEK for backup using the dedicated backupDekWrapKey
 */
export const wrapDEKForBackup = async (
  rawDek: Uint8Array,
  backupDekWrapKey: CryptoKey
): Promise<{ backupWrappedDek: Uint8Array; backupDekIv: Uint8Array }> => {
  const backupDekIv = randomBytes(IV_BYTES);
  const aad = new TextEncoder().encode('backup-dek-wrap:v1.1');
  const cipherBuffer = await subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: backupDekIv, additionalData: aad },
    backupDekWrapKey,
    rawDek
  );
  return {
    backupWrappedDek: new Uint8Array(cipherBuffer),
    backupDekIv,
  };
};

/**
 * Unwrap raw DEK from backup using the dedicated backupDekWrapKey
 */
export const unwrapDEKFromBackup = async (
  backupWrappedDek: Uint8Array,
  backupDekIv: Uint8Array,
  backupDekWrapKey: CryptoKey
): Promise<Uint8Array> => {
  try {
    const aad = new TextEncoder().encode('backup-dek-wrap:v1.1');
    const rawBuffer = await subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: backupDekIv, additionalData: aad },
      backupDekWrapKey,
      backupWrappedDek
    );
    return new Uint8Array(rawBuffer);
  } catch {
    throw new DecryptionAuthError(
      'فشل فك تغليف مفتاح البيانات DEK من النسخة الاحتياطية. إما كلمة مرور النسخة خاطئة أو تم التلاعب بالمفتاح المغلف.'
    );
  }
};

/**
 * Restoring on a destination device: Wrap restored raw DEK under destination Vault KEK,
 * and import fresh non-extractable operational DEK.
 */
export const createDestinationVaultMetaFromRestoredDEK = async (
  rawDek: Uint8Array,
  newVaultPassword: string,
  restoredInvocationCount = 0,
  restoredDekVersion = CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
): Promise<{
  destinationOperationalDek: CryptoKey;
  newVaultSalt: Uint8Array;
  newWrappedDek: Uint8Array;
  newWrappedDekIv: Uint8Array;
  newVerifierIv: Uint8Array;
  newVerifierCiphertext: Uint8Array;
  restoredInvocationCount: number;
  restoredDekVersion: number;
}> => {
  const newVaultSalt = randomBytes(SALT_BYTES);
  const destinationVaultKek = await deriveKEK(newVaultPassword, newVaultSalt);

  // Wrap under destination KEK
  const newWrappedDekIv = randomBytes(IV_BYTES);
  const wrapAad = new TextEncoder().encode('vault-dek-wrap:v1.1');
  const cipherBuffer = await subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: newWrappedDekIv, additionalData: wrapAad },
    destinationVaultKek,
    rawDek
  );

  // Import non-extractable operational DEK for destination
  const destinationOperationalDek = await subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    rawDek,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false, // strictly NON-EXTRACTABLE
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );

  // Derive domain-separated HKDF subkey for atomic session sequence tokens
  const hmacKey = await deriveSequenceHmacKey(rawDek);
  registerSequenceHmacKey(destinationOperationalDek, hmacKey);

  // Generate verifier token for new destination password
  const verifier = await generateVerifier(destinationVaultKek);

  return {
    destinationOperationalDek,
    newVaultSalt,
    newWrappedDek: new Uint8Array(cipherBuffer),
    newWrappedDekIv,
    newVerifierIv: verifier.verifierIv,
    newVerifierCiphertext: verifier.verifierCiphertext,
    restoredInvocationCount,
    restoredDekVersion,
  };
};

/**
 * Encrypt the exported database snapshot payload for .ptbackup
 * Uses Backup Payload Encryption Key with AES-256-GCM.
 * The canonical JSON string of the backup header is passed as Additional Authenticated Data (AAD).
 */
export const encryptBackupPayload = async (
  backupPayloadEncKey: CryptoKey,
  headerAad: Uint8Array,
  databasePayload: string | Uint8Array
): Promise<{ backupDataIv: Uint8Array; ciphertext: Uint8Array }> => {
  const iv = randomBytes(IV_BYTES);
  const dataBytes = typeof databasePayload === 'string'
    ? new TextEncoder().encode(databasePayload)
    : databasePayload;

  const cipherBuffer = await subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: headerAad },
    backupPayloadEncKey,
    dataBytes
  );

  return {
    backupDataIv: iv,
    ciphertext: new Uint8Array(cipherBuffer),
  };
};

/**
 * Decrypt the database snapshot payload from .ptbackup
 * Uses Backup Payload Encryption Key with AES-256-GCM and verifies the header AAD.
 */
export const decryptBackupPayload = async (
  backupPayloadEncKey: CryptoKey,
  headerAad: Uint8Array,
  backupDataIv: Uint8Array,
  ciphertext: Uint8Array
): Promise<string> => {
  try {
    const plainBuffer = await subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: backupDataIv, additionalData: headerAad },
      backupPayloadEncKey,
      ciphertext
    );
    return new TextDecoder().decode(plainBuffer);
  } catch {
    throw new DecryptionAuthError(
      'فشل التحقق من صحة ومصادقة حزمة النسخة الاحتياطية. إما تم تعديل الترويسة أو تلف ملف النسخ أو كلمة المرور غير صحيحة.'
    );
  }
};

/**
 * Validate raw backup string BEFORE JSON.parse to prevent memory exhaustion or malicious inputs.
 */
export const validateRawBackupString = (
  rawStr: string,
  maxSizeBytes = MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES * 2
): void => {
  if (typeof rawStr !== 'string' || !rawStr.trim()) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('ملف النسخة الاحتياطية فارغ أو غير صالح.');
  }
  if (rawStr.length > maxSizeBytes) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(
      `حجم ملف النسخة الخام (${rawStr.length} حرف) يتجاوز الحد الأقصى المسموح به (${maxSizeBytes} بايت) قبل معالجة JSON.`
    );
  }
  if (rawStr.length < 50) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حجم ملف النسخة صغير جداً ولا يمكن أن يكون ملف نسخة صالحاً.');
  }
};

/**
 * Pre-validation of untrusted backup header BEFORE key derivation.
 * Rigorously checks all mandatory fields, types, byte lengths, and exact v1.1 parameters.
 */
export const validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation = (
  untrustedHeader: unknown,
  maxPayloadSizeBytes = MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES
): void => {
  if (!untrustedHeader || typeof untrustedHeader !== 'object' || Array.isArray(untrustedHeader)) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('بنية ترويسة ملف النسخة غير صالحة.');
  }
  const header = untrustedHeader as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (header.magic !== 'PHYSIORECORD_BACKUP_V1.1') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`المعرف السحري غير صالح: ${String(header.magic)}`);
  }
  if (typeof header.exportDateUtc !== 'string' || !header.exportDateUtc || isNaN(Date.parse(header.exportDateUtc))) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('تاريخ التصدير exportDateUtc غير صالح أو مفقود.');
  }
  if (typeof header.clinicTimezone !== 'string' || !header.clinicTimezone.trim()) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('المنطقة الزمنية clinicTimezone غير صالحة أو مفقودة.');
  }
  if (typeof header.schemaVersion !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(header.schemaVersion) || header.schemaVersion < 1) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`إصدار المخطط schemaVersion غير صالح: ${String(header.schemaVersion)}`);
  }
  if (header.kdfAlgorithm !== 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`خوارزمية KDF غير مدعومة: ${String(header.kdfAlgorithm)}`);
  }
  // STRICT v1.1 REQUIREMENT: Reject any iteration count other than 600,000
  if (header.kdfIterations !== 600_000) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(
      `عدد دورات KDF يجب أن يكون 600000 بالضبط لتنسيق v1.1 (المقدم: ${String(header.kdfIterations)}).`
    );
  }
  if (header.keyExpansion !== 'HKDF-SHA256') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`خوارزمية اشتقاق المفاتيح غير مدعومة: ${String(header.keyExpansion)}`);
  }
  if (header.hkdfHash !== 'SHA-256') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`تجزئة HKDF غير مدعومة: ${String(header.hkdfHash)}`);
  }
  if (header.hkdfInfoDekWrap !== 'physiorecord-backup-dek-wrap-v1.1') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`معرف hkdfInfoDekWrap غير مطابق: ${String(header.hkdfInfoDekWrap)}`);
  }
  if (header.hkdfInfoPayloadEnc !== 'physiorecord-backup-payload-enc-v1.1') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`معرف hkdfInfoPayloadEnc غير مطابق: ${String(header.hkdfInfoPayloadEnc)}`);
  }
  if (header.derivedKeyLengthBits !== 256) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`طول المفتاح المشتق derivedKeyLengthBits يجب أن يكون 256.`);
  }

  // Strictly validate byte arrays: length and byte-range [0..255]
  const validateByteArray = (arr: unknown, expectedLen: number, name: string) => {
    if (!arr || typeof arr !== 'object') throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`حقل ${name} مفقود في الترويسة.`);
    const indexable = arr as ArrayLike<unknown>;
    const len = Array.isArray(arr) || arr instanceof Uint8Array ? arr.length : Object.keys(arr).length;
    if (len !== expectedLen) {
      throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`طول ${name} غير مطابق (المطلوب ${expectedLen} بايت، الفعلي ${len}).`);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < expectedLen; i++) {
      const val = indexable[i];
      if (typeof val !== 'number' || val < 0 || val > 255 || !Number.isInteger(val)) {
        throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`قيمة البايت في ${name}[${i}] غير صالحة.`);
      }
    }
  };

  validateByteArray(header.backupSalt, 16, 'ملح النسخة (backupSalt)');
  validateByteArray(header.hkdfSalt, 32, 'ملح HKDF (hkdfSalt)');
  validateByteArray(header.backupDekIv, 12, 'IV مفتاح النسخة (backupDekIv)');
  validateByteArray(header.backupDataIv, 12, 'IV حمولة النسخة (backupDataIv)');

  // backupWrappedDek: AES-256-GCM ciphertext + 16-byte tag (should be at least 16 bytes)
  if (!header.backupWrappedDek || typeof header.backupWrappedDek !== 'object') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حقل المفتاح المغلف backupWrappedDek مفقود.');
  }
  const wrappedLen = Array.isArray(header.backupWrappedDek) || header.backupWrappedDek instanceof Uint8Array
    ? header.backupWrappedDek.length
    : Object.keys(header.backupWrappedDek).length;
  if (wrappedLen < 16) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('طول المفتاح المغلف backupWrappedDek غير صالح.');
  }

  if (typeof header.dekVersion !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(header.dekVersion) || header.dekVersion < 1) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`رقم إصدار المفتاح dekVersion غير صالح: ${String(header.dekVersion)}`);
  }
  if (
    typeof header.encryptionInvocationCount !== 'number' ||
    !Number.isInteger(header.encryptionInvocationCount) ||
    header.encryptionInvocationCount < 0 ||
    header.encryptionInvocationCount > MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK
  ) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(`عداد استدعاءات التشفير encryptionInvocationCount خارج النطاق المسموح.`);
  }
  if (
    typeof header.payloadLength !== 'number' ||
    header.payloadLength <= 0 ||
    header.payloadLength > maxPayloadSizeBytes
  ) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(
      `حجم حمولة النسخة (${String(header.payloadLength)} بايت) غير صالح أو يتجاوز الحد الأقصى (${maxPayloadSizeBytes} بايت).`
    );
  }
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Record Payload Encryption (AES-GCM + Canonical AAD)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Canonical deterministic serialization of Additional Authenticated Data (AAD)
 * Form: TableName:RecordId:ParentId1,ParentId2:recordCryptoVersion:dekVersion
 */
export const buildCanonicalAAD = (
  tableName: string,
  recordId: string,
  parentIds: string[] = [],
  recordCryptoVersion: number = CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  dekVersion: number = CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
): Uint8Array => {
  const canonicalStr = `${tableName}:${recordId}:${parentIds.join(',')}:${recordCryptoVersion}:${dekVersion}`;
  return new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalStr);
};

/**
 * Encrypt record payload using DEK and binding to AAD
 */
export const encryptRecordPayload = async <T>(
  dek: CryptoKey,
  tableName: string,
  recordId: string,
  parentIds: string[] = [],
  data: T,
  recordCryptoVersion: number = CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  dekVersion: number = CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
): Promise<{ iv: Uint8Array; ciphertext: Uint8Array }> => {
  const iv = randomBytes(IV_BYTES);
  const aad = buildCanonicalAAD(tableName, recordId, parentIds, recordCryptoVersion, dekVersion);
  const plaintext = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(data));

  const cipherBuffer = await subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: aad },
    dek,
    plaintext
  );

  return { iv, ciphertext: new Uint8Array(cipherBuffer) };
};

/**
 * Centralized record encryption with MANDATORY transactional DEK counter reservation.
 * Atomically reserves the invocation counter on database.vaultMeta BEFORE encrypting.
 * Throws DekExhaustedError if limit (1,000,000) reached.
 * 
 * Cryptographic & Architectural Guarantees:
 * 1. The counter increment is committed in its own IndexedDB transaction before encryption.
 * 2. If encryption fails, the increment is retained (never decremented).
 * 3. Web Crypto API operations run outside the IndexedDB transaction.
 */
export const encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation = async <T>(
  database: VaultDatabaseHandle,
  dek: CryptoKey,
  tableName: string,
  recordId: string,
  parentIds: string[] = [],
  data: T,
  recordCryptoVersion: number = CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  dekVersion: number = CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
  explicitExpectedGeneration?: number
): Promise<{ iv: Uint8Array; ciphertext: Uint8Array; reservedCount: number }> => {
  // Step 1: Enforce fail-closed key-bound generation fence in the central crypto layer
  const actualBoundGeneration = getDekGeneration(dek);
  if (actualBoundGeneration === undefined) {
    throw new UnboundVaultKeyError(
      `تم رفض تشفير السجل في الجدول "${tableName}": مفتاح التشفير التشغيلي (DEK) غير مرتبط بأي جيل خزينة معتمد (Unbound Vault Key). لا يمكن استهلاك العداد أو تشفير السجل السريري بمفتاح مجهول الجيل.`
    );
  }

  if (
    explicitExpectedGeneration !== undefined &&
    explicitExpectedGeneration !== actualBoundGeneration
  ) {
    throw new VaultGenerationChangedError(
      `تم رفض تشفير السجل في الجدول "${tableName}": الجيل الممرر صراحة (${explicitExpectedGeneration}) لا يطابق جيل الخزينة الفعلي المرتبط بالمفتاح (${actualBoundGeneration}).`
    );
  }

  const reservedCount = await atomicReserveInvocationCount(database, dek, 1);

  // Step 2: Encrypt payload OUTSIDE the IndexedDB transaction
  const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordPayload(
    dek,
    tableName,
    recordId,
    parentIds,
    data,
    recordCryptoVersion,
    dekVersion
  );

  return { iv, ciphertext, reservedCount };
};

/**
 * Decrypt record payload using DEK and verifying AAD
 */
export const decryptRecordPayload = async <T>(
  dek: CryptoKey,
  tableName: string,
  recordId: string,
  parentIds: string[] = [],
  iv: Uint8Array,
  ciphertext: Uint8Array,
  recordCryptoVersion: number = CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  dekVersion: number = CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
): Promise<T> => {
  const aad = buildCanonicalAAD(tableName, recordId, parentIds, recordCryptoVersion, dekVersion);

  try {
    const plainBuffer = await subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: aad },
      dek,
      ciphertext
    );
    const jsonStr = new TextDecoder().decode(plainBuffer);
    return JSON.parse(jsonStr) as T;
  } catch {
    throw new DecryptionAuthError(
      `فشل التحقق من مصادقة التشفير للسجل ${recordId} في جدول ${tableName}. إما تم التلاعب به أو تعديل معرّفاته أو رقم إصدار المفتاح (dekVersion).`
    );
  }
};

/**
 * Generate password verifier token
 */
export const generateVerifier = async (
  kek: CryptoKey
): Promise<{ verifierIv: Uint8Array; verifierCiphertext: Uint8Array }> => {
  const iv = randomBytes(IV_BYTES);
  const plaintext = new TextEncoder().encode(VERIFIER_PLAINTEXT);
  const cipherBuffer = await subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv },
    kek,
    plaintext
  );
  return {
    verifierIv: iv,
    verifierCiphertext: new Uint8Array(cipherBuffer),
  };
};

/**
 * Test whether candidate KEK correctly decrypts verifier token
 */
export const checkVerifier = async (
  kek: CryptoKey,
  verifierIv: Uint8Array,
  verifierCiphertext: Uint8Array
): Promise<boolean> => {
  try {
    const plainBuffer = await subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: verifierIv },
      kek,
      verifierCiphertext
    );
    const val = new TextDecoder().decode(plainBuffer);
    return val === VERIFIER_PLAINTEXT;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
};

/**
 * Check whether cumulative invocations approach rotation threshold
 */
export const checkDekRotationThreshold = (
  invocationCount: number,
  threshold = CONSERVATIVE_ROTATION_THRESHOLD
): { needsRotation: boolean; currentCount: number; remaining: number } => {
  const needsRotation = invocationCount >= threshold;
  const remaining = Math.max(0, threshold - invocationCount);
  return { needsRotation, currentCount: invocationCount, remaining };
};

/**
 * Reproducible PBKDF2 Benchmark Runner
 */
export const benchmarkKdf = async (
  iterations = DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
  sampleCount = 5
): Promise<{ samples: number[]; medianMs: number; worstMs: number }> => {
  const testPassword = 'Benchmarking_Secret_Password_123!';
  const testSalt = randomBytes(SALT_BYTES);
  const samples: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < sampleCount; i++) {
    const t0 = performance.now();
    await deriveKEK(testPassword, testSalt, iterations);
    const t1 = performance.now();
    samples.push(Math.round(t1 - t0));
  }

  const sorted = [...samples].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const medianMs = sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length / 2)];
  const worstMs = sorted[sorted.length - 1];

  return { samples, medianMs, worstMs };
};

/**
 * Transactionally reserve and commit a DEK usage counter increment
 * BEFORE an AES-GCM encryption operation.
 * Uses a Dexie readwrite transaction on vaultMeta to serialize concurrent tabs/operations.
 * If encryption or storage fails subsequently, this increment is retained (never decremented).
 * 
 * Enforces MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK (1,000,000 invocations).
 * If the limit would be exceeded, rejects the operation and throws DekExhaustedError.
 */
export const atomicReserveInvocationCount = async (
  database: VaultDatabaseHandle,
  dek: CryptoKey,
  count = 1
): Promise<number> => {
  // Fail-Closed: Strictly require a valid CryptoKey object
  if (!dek || typeof dek !== 'object') {
    throw new UnboundVaultKeyError(
      'تم رفض حجز عداد التشفير: لم يتم تمرير كائن مفتاح تشفير تشغيلي صالح (dek is not a valid CryptoKey). لا يمكن حجز عداد التشفير بدون مفتاح DEK موثوق.'
    );
  }

  // 1. Extract generation strictly via getDekGeneration(dek) from WeakMap
  const boundGeneration = getDekGeneration(dek);
  if (boundGeneration === undefined) {
    throw new UnboundVaultKeyError(
      'تم رفض حجز عداد التشفير: مفتاح التشفير التشغيلي (DEK) غير مرتبط بأي جيل خزينة معتمد (Unbound Vault Key). لا يمكن حجز عداد التشفير بمفتاح مجهول الجيل.'
    );
  }

  // 2. Open exclusive atomic IndexedDB readwrite transaction
  return await database.transaction('rw', database.vaultMeta, async () => {
    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    if (!meta) throw new LockedVaultError('الخزينة غير مهيأة.');

    // 3. Vault Generation Epoch Fence: compare WeakMap-extracted generation with database vaultMeta.vaultGeneration
    const currentGen = meta.vaultGeneration ?? 1;
    if (currentGen !== boundGeneration) {
      throw new VaultGenerationChangedError(
        `تم رفض العملية: جيل الخزينة في قاعدة البيانات (${currentGen}) لا يطابق جيل المفتاح التشغيلي المستخدم (${boundGeneration}) نتيجة استعادة كاملة في تبويب آخر. يرجى إعادة فتح الخزينة بالمفتاح الصحيح.`
      );
    }

    const current = meta.encryptionInvocationCount || 0;
    if (current + count > MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK) {
      throw new DekExhaustedError(
        `تم الوصول إلى الحد الأقصى لاستخدام مفتاح التشفير (${MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK} عملية). يرجى إجراء تدوير آمن لمفتاح DEK قبل المتابعة.`
      );
    }
    const newCount = current + count;
    await database.vaultMeta.update('auth', {
      encryptionInvocationCount: newCount,
    });
    return newCount;
  });
};

export const CONSTANTS = {
  SALT_BYTES,
  HKDF_SALT_BYTES,
  IV_BYTES,
  DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
  MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK,
  CONSERVATIVE_ROTATION_THRESHOLD,
  MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES,
};

// Safely register test hooks for test environments only (isolated/no-op in production)
registerTestHooks({
  generateAndWrapOperationalDEK,
  deriveKEK,
  encryptRecordPayload,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation,
  atomicReserveInvocationCount,
  randomBytes,
  bindDekToGeneration,
  getDekGeneration,
  UnboundVaultKeyError,
});

import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as path from 'node:path';
import {
  deriveKEK,
  generateAndWrapOperationalDEK,
  unwrapOperationalDEK,
  generateVerifier,
  checkVerifier,

  __dirname,
  'fixtures',
  'v1_1_vault_and_backup.fixture.json'

interface CommittedV11Fixture {

  backupPassword: string;

      iv: number[];

      iv: number[];

    patient: Record<string, unknown>;
    episode: Record<string, unknown>;
    session: Record<string, unknown>;
