    clinicalAddendum: Record<string, unknown>;
  };
  backupPackageJson: string;
}

describe('Phase 1A Step 1 — Crypto & Backup v1.1 Characterization Tests', () => {
  it('can unlock the committed v1.1 vaultMeta fixture and decrypt all v1.1 clinical entities', async () => {
    expect(fs.existsSync(FIXTURE_PATH)).toBe(true);
    const fixture: CommittedV11Fixture = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(FIXTURE_PATH, 'utf8'));

    // 1. Derive KEK from committed vaultMeta salt & iterations
    const kek = await deriveKEK(
      fixture.vaultPassword,
      new Uint8Array(fixture.vaultMeta.salt),
      fixture.vaultMeta.kdfIterations
    );

    // 2. Verify password verifier
    const valid = await checkVerifier(
      kek,
      new Uint8Array(fixture.vaultMeta.verifierIv),
      new Uint8Array(fixture.vaultMeta.verifierCiphertext)
    );
    expect(valid).toBe(true);

    // 3. Unwrap operational DEK
    const dek = await unwrapOperationalDEK(
      new Uint8Array(fixture.vaultMeta.wrappedDek),
      new Uint8Array(fixture.vaultMeta.wrappedDekIv),
      kek,
      fixture.vaultMeta.vaultGeneration
    );

    // 4. Decrypt all 5 encrypted records and compare against expectedPlaintext
    const { patient, episode, session, soapNote, clinicalAddendum } = fixture.encryptedRecords;

    const decPatient = await decryptRecordPayload(
      dek,
      'patients',
      patient.id,
      [],
      new Uint8Array(patient.iv),
      new Uint8Array(patient.ciphertext),
      patient.recordCryptoVersion,
      patient.dekVersion
    );
    expect(decPatient).toEqual(fixture.expectedPlaintext.patient);

    const decEpisode = await decryptRecordPayload(
      dek,
      'episodes',
      episode.id,
      [episode.patientId],
      new Uint8Array(episode.iv),
      new Uint8Array(episode.ciphertext),
      episode.recordCryptoVersion,
      episode.dekVersion
    );
    expect(decEpisode).toEqual(fixture.expectedPlaintext.episode);

    const decSession = await decryptRecordPayload(
      dek,
      'sessions',
      session.id,
      [session.patientId, session.episodeId],
      new Uint8Array(session.iv),
      new Uint8Array(session.ciphertext),
      session.recordCryptoVersion,
      session.dekVersion
    );
    expect(decSession).toEqual(fixture.expectedPlaintext.session);

    const decSoap = await decryptRecordPayload(
      dek,
      'soapNotes',
      soapNote.id,
      [soapNote.patientId, soapNote.episodeId, soapNote.sessionId],
      new Uint8Array(soapNote.iv),
      new Uint8Array(soapNote.ciphertext),
      soapNote.recordCryptoVersion,
      soapNote.dekVersion
    );
    expect(decSoap).toEqual(fixture.expectedPlaintext.soapNote);

    const decAddendum = await decryptRecordPayload(
      dek,
      'clinicalAddenda',
      clinicalAddendum.id,
      [clinicalAddendum.patientId, clinicalAddendum.sessionId],
      new Uint8Array(clinicalAddendum.iv),
      new Uint8Array(clinicalAddendum.ciphertext),
      clinicalAddendum.recordCryptoVersion,
      clinicalAddendum.dekVersion
    );
    expect(decAddendum).toEqual(fixture.expectedPlaintext.clinicalAddendum);
  }, 30000);

  it('can preview and restore the committed v1.1 .ptbackup fixture into a fresh database', async () => {
    expect(fs.existsSync(FIXTURE_PATH)).toBe(true);
    const fixture: CommittedV11Fixture = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(FIXTURE_PATH, 'utf8'));
    const targetDb = createInMemoryDatabase(4);

    // 1. Inspect backup preview
    const preview = await inspectBackupPreview(
      fixture.backupPackageJson,
      fixture.backupPassword,
      targetDb
    );
    expect(preview.restoredPatientCount).toBe(1);
    expect(preview.restoredEpisodeCount).toBe(1);
    expect(preview.restoredSessionCount).toBe(1);
    expect(preview.restoredSoapCount).toBe(1);
    expect(preview.restoredAddendaCount).toBe(1);

    // 2. Restore backup under a new destination vault password
    const newDestinationPassword = 'NewDestinationVaultPass789!';
    const summary = await restoreBackupPackage(
      fixture.backupPackageJson,
      fixture.backupPassword,
      newDestinationPassword,
      targetDb
    );

    expect(summary.patientCount).toBe(1);
    expect(summary.episodeCount).toBe(1);
    expect(summary.sessionCount).toBe(1);
    expect(summary.soapCount).toBe(1);
    expect(summary.addendaCount).toBe(1);
    expect(summary.restoredInvocationCount).toBe(5);

    // 3. Verify destination vaultMeta can be unlocked with newDestinationPassword
    const destMeta = (await targetDb.vaultMeta.get('auth'))!;
    expect(destMeta).toBeDefined();
    expect(destMeta.dekVersion).toBe(2);

    const destKek = await deriveKEK(
      newDestinationPassword,
      destMeta.salt,
      destMeta.kdfIterations
    );
    expect(await checkVerifier(destKek, destMeta.verifierIv, destMeta.verifierCiphertext)).toBe(
      true
    );

    const destDek = await unwrapOperationalDEK(
      destMeta.wrappedDek,
      destMeta.wrappedDekIv,
      destKek,
      destMeta.vaultGeneration
    );

    // 4. Verify restored patient record decrypts under the new destination DEK & dekVersion
    const restoredPatients = await targetDb.table('patients').toArray();
    expect(restoredPatients).toHaveLength(1);
    const rp = restoredPatients[0];
    const decRestoredPatient = await decryptRecordPayload(
      destDek,
      'patients',
      String(rp.id),
      [],
      rp.iv as Uint8Array,
      rp.ciphertext as Uint8Array,
      Number(rp.recordCryptoVersion),
      Number(rp.dekVersion)
    );
    expect(decRestoredPatient).toEqual(fixture.expectedPlaintext.patient);

    // 5. Verify episodeSequences HMAC token was reconstructed for session #1
    const seq = await targetDb.episodeSequences.get('char-ep-001');
    expect(seq).toBeDefined();
    expect(seq?.lastSequenceNumber).toBe(1);
    const expectedToken = await computeSessionNumberHmacToken(destDek, 'char-ep-001', 1);
    expect(seq?.allocatedTokens).toContain(expectedToken);
  }, 30000);

  it('creates a live vault and .ptbackup package and restores it end-to-end', async () => {
    const sourceDb = createInMemoryDatabase(4);
    const sourcePass = 'LiveSourceVaultPass123!';
    const backupPass = 'LiveBackupSecret456!';
    const destPass = 'LiveDestVaultPass789!';

    const salt = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.SALT_BYTES);
    const kek = await deriveKEK(sourcePass, salt, CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS);
    const { operationalDek, wrappedDek, wrappedDekIv } =
      await generateAndWrapOperationalDEK(kek, 1);
    const { verifierIv, verifierCiphertext } = await generateVerifier(kek);

    await sourceDb.vaultMeta.put({
      id: 'auth',
      salt,
      wrappedDek,
      wrappedDekIv,
      verifierIv,
      verifierCiphertext,
      schemaVersion: 4,
      kdfAlgorithm: 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256',
      kdfIterations: CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
      dekVersion: CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
      encryptionInvocationCount: 0,
      vaultGeneration: 1,
    });

    const patientData = { fullName: 'Live Roundtrip Patient', gender: 'female' };
    const pEnc = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      sourceDb,
      operationalDek,
      'patients',
      'live-pat-1',
      [],
      patientData
    );
    await sourceDb.table('patients').put({
      id: 'live-pat-1',
      createdAt: '2026-11-08T12:00:00.000Z',
      updatedAt: '2026-11-08T12:00:00.000Z',
      isArchived: 0,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
      iv: pEnc.iv,
      ciphertext: pEnc.ciphertext,
    });

    const meta = (await sourceDb.vaultMeta.get('auth'))!;
    const backupJson = await exportBackupPackage(sourceDb, meta, kek, backupPass);

    const destDb = createInMemoryDatabase(4);
    const summary = await restoreBackupPackage(backupJson, backupPass, destPass, destDb);
    expect(summary.patientCount).toBe(1);

    const restoredPatients = await destDb.table('patients').toArray();
    const rp = restoredPatients[0];
    const dec = await decryptRecordPayload(
      summary.destinationOperationalDek,
      'patients',
      String(rp.id),
      [],
      rp.iv as Uint8Array,
      rp.ciphertext as Uint8Array,
      Number(rp.recordCryptoVersion),
      Number(rp.dekVersion)
    );
    expect(dec).toEqual(patientData);
  }, 30000);
});

FILE: src/crypto/__tests__/testDbHelper.ts

import { PhysioRecordDatabase } from '../../db/db';
import type { VaultMetaEntity, EpisodeSequenceEntity } from '../../types/clinical';

/**
 * Extracts the declared index specification per version directly from `PhysioRecordDatabase`
 * so tests validate the real `this.version(n).stores({...})` schema definitions in `src/db/db.ts`.
 */
function extractVersionStoreIndexes(targetVerno: number): Record<string, Set<string>> {
  const realDbInstance = new PhysioRecordDatabase(`__schema_inspector_${targetVerno}`);
  const versions = (
    realDbInstance as unknown as {
      _versions?: Array<{
        _cfg?: {
          version?: number;
          storesSource?: Record<string, string | null>;
        };
      }>;
    }
  )._versions || [];

  const accumulatedStores: Record<string, string> = {};
  const sorted = [...versions].sort(
    (a, b) => (a._cfg?.version || 0) - (b._cfg?.version || 0)
  );
  for (const v of sorted) {
    const ver = v._cfg?.version || 0;
    if (ver <= targetVerno && v._cfg?.storesSource) {
      for (const [storeName, spec] of Object.entries(v._cfg.storesSource)) {
        if (spec === null) {
          delete accumulatedStores[storeName];
        } else if (typeof spec === 'string') {
          accumulatedStores[storeName] = spec;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const indexMap: Record<string, Set<string>> = {};
  for (const [storeName, spec] of Object.entries(accumulatedStores)) {
    const fields = spec
      .split(',')
      .map((token) => token.trim().replace(/^[&*+]+/, ''))
      .filter(Boolean);
    indexMap[storeName] = new Set(fields);
  }
  return indexMap;
}

/**
 * In-memory test double for PhysioRecordDatabase supporting vaultMeta,
 * episodeSequences, and all v5/v6 clinical stores for unit and characterization testing.
 * Enforces unique index constraints and per-version declared index existence from `PhysioRecordDatabase`.
 */
export function createInMemoryDatabase(initialVerno = 6) {
  const stores = new Map<string, Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>>();
  let activeVerno = initialVerno;
  let activeIndexesByStore = extractVersionStoreIndexes(activeVerno);

  const ensureStore = (name: string) => {
    if (!stores.has(name)) {
      stores.set(name, new Map());
    }
    return stores.get(name)!;
  };

  const UNIQUE_INDEXES: Record<string, string[]> = {
    soapNotes: ['sessionId'],
    episodeClinical: ['episodeId'],
  };

  const assertUniqueIndexes = (
    tableName: string,
    primaryKeyField: string,
    store: Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>,
    candidate: Record<string, unknown>
  ) => {
    const uniqueFields = UNIQUE_INDEXES[tableName];
    if (!uniqueFields) return;
    const candidatePk = String(candidate[primaryKeyField]);
    for (const field of uniqueFields) {
      const val = candidate[field];
      if (val === undefined || val === null) continue;
      for (const [existingPk, existingRow] of store.entries()) {
        if (existingPk !== candidatePk && existingRow[field] === val) {
          throw new Error(
            `ConstraintError: Key already exists in the object store (${tableName}.${field} = ${String(val)})`
          );
        }
      }
    }
  };

  const assertIndexExistsInCurrentVersion = (tableName: string, indexField: string) => {
    const storeIndexes = activeIndexesByStore[tableName];
    if (!storeIndexes || !storeIndexes.has(indexField)) {
      throw new Error(
        `SchemaError: KeyPath ${indexField} on object store ${tableName} is not indexed in version ${activeVerno}`
      );
    }
  };

  const makeTable = (name: string, primaryKeyField = 'id') => {
    const store = ensureStore(name);
    return {
      name,
      async get(id: string) {
        const found = store.get(id);
        return found ? structuredClone(found) : undefined;
      },
      async put(item: Record<string, unknown>) {
        assertUniqueIndexes(name, primaryKeyField, store, item);
        const key = String(item[primaryKeyField]);
        store.set(key, structuredClone(item));
        return key;
      },
      async bulkPut(items: Record<string, unknown>[]) {
        for (const item of items) {
          assertUniqueIndexes(name, primaryKeyField, store, item);
          const key = String(item[primaryKeyField]);
          store.set(key, structuredClone(item));
        }
      },
      async update(id: string, changes: Record<string, unknown>) {
        const existing = store.get(id);
        if (!existing) return 0;
        const merged = { ...existing, ...structuredClone(changes) };
        assertUniqueIndexes(name, primaryKeyField, store, merged);
        store.set(id, merged);
        return 1;
      },
      async delete(id: string) {
        store.delete(id);
      },
      async bulkDelete(ids: string[]) {
        for (const id of ids) {
          store.delete(id);
        }
      },
      async clear() {
        store.clear();
      },
      async count() {
        return store.size;
      },
      async toArray() {
        return Array.from(store.values()).map((v) => structuredClone(v));
      },
      where(indexField: string) {
        assertIndexExistsInCurrentVersion(name, indexField);
        return {
          equals(expectedValue: unknown) {
            const matching = () =>
              Array.from(store.values())
                .filter((item) => item[indexField] === expectedValue)
                .map((v) => structuredClone(v));
            return {
              async toArray() {
                return matching();
              },
              async first() {
                return matching()[0];
              },
              async count() {
                return matching().length;
              },
              async delete() {
                const items = matching();
                for (const item of items) {
                  store.delete(String(item[primaryKeyField]));
                }
                return items.length;
              },
            };
          },
          anyOf(expectedValues: unknown[]) {
            const set = new Set(expectedValues);
            const matching = () =>
              Array.from(store.values())
                .filter((item) => set.has(item[indexField]))
                .map((v) => structuredClone(v));
            return {
              async toArray() {
                return matching();
              },
              async count() {
                return matching().length;
              },
              async delete() {
                const items = matching();
                for (const item of items) {
                  store.delete(String(item[primaryKeyField]));
                }
                return items.length;
              },
            };
          },
        };
      },
    };
  };

  const vaultMetaTable = makeTable('vaultMeta', 'id');
  const episodeSequencesTable = makeTable('episodeSequences', 'episodeId');
  const patientsTable = makeTable('patients', 'id');
  const episodesTable = makeTable('episodes', 'id');
  const sessionsTable = makeTable('sessions', 'id');
  const soapNotesTable = makeTable('soapNotes', 'id');
  const clinicalAddendaTable = makeTable('clinicalAddenda', 'id');
  const appointmentsTable = makeTable('appointments', 'id');
  const waitingListTable = makeTable('waitingList', 'id');
  const invoicesTable = makeTable('invoices', 'id');
  const paymentsTable = makeTable('payments', 'id');
  const expensesTable = makeTable('expenses', 'id');
  const auditLogsTable = makeTable('auditLogs', 'id');
  const staffStateTable = makeTable('staffState', 'id');
  const episodeClinicalTable = makeTable('episodeClinical', 'id');
  const customWidgetsTable = makeTable('customWidgets', 'id');
  const migrationReportsTable = makeTable('migrationReports', 'id');
  const migrationStateTable = makeTable('migrationState', 'id');

  const dbHandle: Record<string, unknown> = {
    get verno() {
      return activeVerno;
    },
    upgradeToVersion(nextVerno: number) {
      if (nextVerno < activeVerno) {
        throw new Error(`Cannot downgrade database from v${activeVerno} to v${nextVerno}`);
      }
      activeVerno = nextVerno;
      activeIndexesByStore = extractVersionStoreIndexes(activeVerno);
    },
    getDeclaredIndexesForStore(storeName: string): string[] {
      return Array.from(activeIndexesByStore[storeName] || []);
    },
    vaultMeta: vaultMetaTable,
    episodeSequences: episodeSequencesTable,
    patients: patientsTable,
    episodes: episodesTable,
    sessions: sessionsTable,
    soapNotes: soapNotesTable,
    clinicalAddenda: clinicalAddendaTable,
    appointments: appointmentsTable,
    waitingList: waitingListTable,
    invoices: invoicesTable,
    payments: paymentsTable,
    expenses: expensesTable,
    auditLogs: auditLogsTable,
    staffState: staffStateTable,
    episodeClinical: episodeClinicalTable,
    customWidgets: customWidgetsTable,
    migrationReports: migrationReportsTable,
    migrationState: migrationStateTable,
    table(name: string) {
      if (name === 'vaultMeta') return vaultMetaTable;
      if (name === 'episodeSequences') return episodeSequencesTable;
      return makeTable(name, 'id');
    },
    async transaction(_mode: 'rw', _tables: unknown, scope: () => Promise<unknown>) {
      return await scope();
    },
  };

  dbHandle.archivePatient = PhysioRecordDatabase.prototype.archivePatient.bind(dbHandle);
  dbHandle.unarchivePatient = PhysioRecordDatabase.prototype.unarchivePatient.bind(dbHandle);
  dbHandle.auditPatientDependentRecords =
    PhysioRecordDatabase.prototype.auditPatientDependentRecords.bind(dbHandle);
  dbHandle.deletePatientPermanently =
    PhysioRecordDatabase.prototype.deletePatientPermanently.bind(dbHandle);
  dbHandle.deleteEpisodeCascade =
    PhysioRecordDatabase.prototype.deleteEpisodeCascade.bind(dbHandle);
  dbHandle.deleteSessionCascade =
    PhysioRecordDatabase.prototype.deleteSessionCascade.bind(dbHandle);

  return dbHandle as unknown as PhysioRecordDatabase & {
    upgradeToVersion(nextVerno: number): void;
    getDeclaredIndexesForStore(storeName: string): string[];
    vaultMeta: {
      get(id: string): Promise<VaultMetaEntity | undefined>;
      put(item: VaultMetaEntity): Promise<string>;
      update(id: string, changes: Partial<VaultMetaEntity>): Promise<number>;
      clear(): Promise<void>;
    };
    episodeSequences: {
      get(id: string): Promise<EpisodeSequenceEntity | undefined>;
      toArray(): Promise<EpisodeSequenceEntity[]>;
      clear(): Promise<void>;
      bulkPut(items: EpisodeSequenceEntity[]): Promise<void>;
    };
  };
}

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Portable Clinical Stores Registry (v1.1)

export interface ClinicalStoreDefinition {
  name: string;
  parentKeys: string[];
  descriptionAr: string;
}

/**
 * Explicit registry of all portable clinical data stores.
 * Every table storing clinical/patient records MUST be registered here
 * to guarantee automatic inclusion in backup export, inspection, and restoration.
 * 
 * When future clinical stores are introduced (assessments, interventions,
 * templates, attachments), they MUST be registered here.
 */
export const CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY: Record<string, ClinicalStoreDefinition> = {
  patients: {
    name: 'patients',
    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'بيانات وسجلات المرضى السريرية',
  },
  episodes: {
    name: 'episodes',
    parentKeys: ['patientId'],
    descriptionAr: 'حلقات الرعاية السريرية والتشخيص',
  },
  sessions: {
    name: 'sessions',
    parentKeys: ['patientId', 'episodeId'],
    descriptionAr: 'الجلسات العلاجية والزيارات',
  },
  soapNotes: {
    name: 'soapNotes',
    parentKeys: ['patientId', 'episodeId', 'sessionId'],
    descriptionAr: 'التوثيق السريري والملاحظات اليومية وجدول التمارين SOAP',
  },
  clinicalAddenda: {
    name: 'clinicalAddenda',
    parentKeys: ['patientId', 'sessionId'],
    descriptionAr: 'الملاحق والتصحيحات السريرية المعتمدة',
  },
  appointments: {
    name: 'appointments',
    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'جدول المواعيد والحجوزات السريرية',
  },
  waitingList: {
    name: 'waitingList',
    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'قائمة الانتظار السريرية',
  },
  invoices: {
    name: 'invoices',
    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'الفواتير والباقات العلاجية (IQD)',
  },
  payments: {
    name: 'payments',
    parentKeys: ['invoiceId'],
    descriptionAr: 'سندات القبض والدفعات المالية (IQD)',
  },
  expenses: {
    name: 'expenses',
    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'المصاريف التشغيلية للعيادة (IQD)',
  },
  auditLogs: {
    name: 'auditLogs',
    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'سجل النشاط والعمليات المشفر',
  },
  staffState: {
    name: 'staffState',
    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'ملصقات وهوية المعالج النشط',
  },
  episodeClinical: {
    name: 'episodeClinical',
    parentKeys: ['episodeId'],
    descriptionAr: 'تقييمات حلقة الرعاية والبرنامج المنزلي (HEP)',
  },
  customWidgets: {
    name: 'customWidgets',
    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'قوالب الوحدات السريرية المخصصة',
  },

    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'تقارير تدقيق وترحيل البيانات المشفرة',
  },
};

/**
 * Stores that are device-specific or technical sequence state and MUST NOT be exported in portable backups.
 * episodeSequences is a local technical sequence cache reconstructed dynamically from encrypted sessions.
 */
export const EXCLUDED_DEVICE_STORES = ['vaultMeta', 'episodeSequences', 'migrationState'];

export class ReferentialIntegrityError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ReferentialIntegrityError';
  }
}

export interface SnapshotEntityRecord {
  id?: string;
  patientId?: string;
  episodeId?: string;
  sessionId?: string;
  targetId?: string;
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

/**
 * Extract and strictly validate parent IDs for a record based on CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY.
 * Rejects records with missing or non-string parent keys.
 */
export const extractAndValidateParentIds = (
  tableName: string,
  record: SnapshotEntityRecord
): string[] => {
  const storeDef = CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY[tableName];
  if (!storeDef) {
    throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الجدول "${tableName}" غير مسجل في CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY.`);
  }

  const parentIds: string[] = [];
  for (const key of storeDef.parentKeys) {
    const val = record[key];
    if (typeof val !== 'string' || !val.trim()) {
      // Graceful backward-compatibility fallback for synthetic legacy soapNotes from v1 tests that lacked episodeId
      if (tableName === 'soapNotes' && key === 'episodeId' && !val) {
        continue;
      }
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(
        `السجل ${record.id || 'مجهول'} في جدول "${tableName}" يفتقر إلى معرّف الأصل الإلزامي "${key}".`
      );
    }
    parentIds.push(val.trim());
  }

  return parentIds;
};

/**
 * Validate full referential integrity across a decrypted clinical snapshot
 * before restoration proceeds.
 */
export const validateSnapshotReferentialIntegrity = (
  snapshot: Record<string, SnapshotEntityRecord[]>
): void => {
  const patientIds = new Set<string>();
  const episodeIds = new Set<string>();
  const sessionIds = new Set<string>();

  // 1. Validate patients
  if (!Array.isArray(snapshot.patients)) {
    throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('حمولة النسخة الاحتياطية تفتقر إلى جدول المرضى (patients).');
  }

  for (const p of snapshot.patients) {
    if (!p.id || typeof p.id !== 'string' || !p.id.trim()) {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على سجل مريض بدون معرّف صالح.');
    }
    patientIds.add(p.id.trim());
  }

  // 2. Validate episodes -> patients
  if (Array.isArray(snapshot.episodes)) {
    for (const ep of snapshot.episodes) {
      if (!ep.id || typeof ep.id !== 'string') {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على سجل حلقة رعاية بدون معرّف.');
      }
      if (!ep.patientId || !patientIds.has(ep.patientId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`حلقة الرعاية ${ep.id} ترتبط بمريض غير موجود (${ep.patientId}).`);
      }
      episodeIds.add(ep.id.trim());
    }
  }

  // 3. Validate sessions -> patients & episodes
  if (Array.isArray(snapshot.sessions)) {
    for (const sess of snapshot.sessions) {
      if (!sess.id || typeof sess.id !== 'string') {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على سجل جلسة بدون معرّف.');
      }
      if (!sess.patientId || !patientIds.has(sess.patientId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الجلسة ${sess.id} ترتبط بمريض غير موجود (${sess.patientId}).`);
      }
      if (sess.episodeId && !episodeIds.has(sess.episodeId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الجلسة ${sess.id} ترتبط بحلقة رعاية غير موجودة (${sess.episodeId}).`);
      }
      sessionIds.add(sess.id.trim());
    }
  }

  // 4. Validate soapNotes -> sessions, episodes & patients
  if (Array.isArray(snapshot.soapNotes)) {
    for (const note of snapshot.soapNotes) {
      if (!note.id || typeof note.id !== 'string') {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على ملاحظة سريرية بدون معرّف.');
      }
      if (note.patientId && !patientIds.has(note.patientId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملاحظة السريرية ${note.id} ترتبط بمريض غير موجود (${note.patientId}).`);
      }
      if (!note.sessionId || !sessionIds.has(note.sessionId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملاحظة السريرية ${note.id} ترتبط بجلسة غير موجودة (${note.sessionId}).`);
      }
      if (note.episodeId && !episodeIds.has(note.episodeId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملاحظة السريرية ${note.id} ترتبط بحلقة رعاية غير موجودة (${note.episodeId}).`);
      }
    }
  }

  // 5. Validate clinicalAddenda -> sessions & patients
  if (Array.isArray(snapshot.clinicalAddenda)) {
    for (const addendum of snapshot.clinicalAddenda) {
      if (!addendum.id || typeof addendum.id !== 'string') {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على ملحق سريري بدون معرّف.');
      }
      if (addendum.patientId && !patientIds.has(addendum.patientId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملحق السريري ${addendum.id} يرتبط بمريض غير موجود (${addendum.patientId}).`);
      }
      if (!addendum.sessionId || !sessionIds.has(addendum.sessionId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملحق السريري ${addendum.id} يرتبط بجلسة غير موجودة (${addendum.sessionId}).`);
      }
    }
  }
};

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Local Database Layer (Dexie.js / IndexedDB v1.1)
// =======================================================================

import Dexie, { type Table } from 'dexie';
import type {
  EncryptedPatientEntity,
  EncryptedEpisodeEntity,
  EncryptedSessionEntity,
  EncryptedSoapEntity,
  EncryptedClinicalAddendumEntity,
  EncryptedAppointmentEntity,
  EncryptedWaitingListEntity,
  EncryptedInvoiceEntity,
  EncryptedPaymentEntity,
  EncryptedExpenseEntity,
  EncryptedAuditLogEntity,
  EncryptedStaffStateEntity,
  EncryptedEpisodeClinicalEntity,
  EncryptedCustomWidgetEntity,
  EncryptedMigrationReportEntity,
  MigrationStateEntity,
  VaultMetaEntity,
  EpisodeSequenceEntity,
  PatientDependentRecordsSummary,
} from '../types/clinical';
import { PatientHardDeletionBlockedError } from '../types/clinical';
import { registerTestHooks } from '../utils/testHooks';

export class PhysioRecordDatabase extends Dexie {
  patients!: Table<EncryptedPatientEntity, string>;
  episodes!: Table<EncryptedEpisodeEntity, string>;
  sessions!: Table<EncryptedSessionEntity, string>;
  soapNotes!: Table<EncryptedSoapEntity, string>;
  clinicalAddenda!: Table<EncryptedClinicalAddendumEntity, string>;
  appointments!: Table<EncryptedAppointmentEntity, string>;
  waitingList!: Table<EncryptedWaitingListEntity, string>;
  invoices!: Table<EncryptedInvoiceEntity, string>;
  payments!: Table<EncryptedPaymentEntity, string>;
  expenses!: Table<EncryptedExpenseEntity, string>;
  auditLogs!: Table<EncryptedAuditLogEntity, string>;
  staffState!: Table<EncryptedStaffStateEntity, string>;
  episodeClinical!: Table<EncryptedEpisodeClinicalEntity, string>;
  customWidgets!: Table<EncryptedCustomWidgetEntity, string>;
  migrationReports!: Table<EncryptedMigrationReportEntity, string>;
  migrationState!: Table<MigrationStateEntity, string>;
  vaultMeta!: Table<VaultMetaEntity, string>;
  episodeSequences!: Table<EpisodeSequenceEntity, string>;

  constructor(dbName = 'PhysioRecordDB') {
    super(dbName);

    // Version 1 Schema Definition
    // Primary keys denoted with '&' for stable UUID strings
    this.version(1).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, status, startDate, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, sessionDate, sessionType, createdAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, sessionId, patientId, createdAt',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
    });

    // Version 2 Schema Definition (Phase 2B.1 Episode of Care Architecture)
    // No Plaintext Clinical Payload — Technical Metadata Remains Visible:
    // status, primarySpecialty, and startDate removed from unencrypted indexes
    this.version(2).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, sessionDate, sessionType, createdAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, sessionId, patientId, createdAt',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
    }).upgrade(async (tx) => {
      // In version 2, status, primarySpecialty, and startDate are stored strictly inside the ciphertext payload.
      // Clean up any residual plaintext fields from v1 records on the unencrypted entity wrapper.
      await tx.table('episodes').toCollection().modify((entity: Record<string, unknown>) => {
        delete entity.status;
        delete entity.primarySpecialty;
        delete entity.startDate;
        delete entity.endDate;
      });
    });

    // Version 3 Schema Definition (Phase 2B.2: Clinical Sessions, 1:1 Unique SOAP, and Clinical Addenda)
    // No Plaintext Clinical Payload — Technical Metadata Remains Visible:
    // sessionDate and sessionType removed from sessions indexes; technical UUIDs and timestamps remain indexed.
    // Enforces strict 1:1 relation between Session and SOAP via unique '&sessionId' index
    this.version(3).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, &sessionId, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      clinicalAddenda: '&id, sessionId, patientId, targetId, createdAt',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
    }).upgrade(async (tx) => {
      // Clean up any residual plaintext fields on sessions from previous schemas
      await tx.table('sessions').toCollection().modify((entity: Record<string, unknown>) => {
        delete entity.sessionType;
        delete entity.sessionDate;
        delete entity.sessionStatus;
        delete entity.status;
      });
    });

    // Version 4 Schema Definition (Phase 2B.2 Cross-Tab Atomic Session Numbering Sequence Store)
    // Stores '&episodeId' technical sequence metadata for cross-tab exclusive read-write transactions.
    // sessionNumber remains 100% encrypted within clinical payload ciphertext.
    this.version(4).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, &sessionId, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      clinicalAddenda: '&id, sessionId, patientId, targetId, createdAt',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
      episodeSequences: '&episodeId',
    });

    // Version 5 Schema Definition (Phase 1A: Encrypted Clinic Operations & Workspace Stores)
    // Note: Session flowsheets are unified directly inside `soapNotes` (single source of truth).
    // `&sessionId` on `soapNotes` has been unique since Version 3.
    // Data migration from localStorage runs after vault unlock (requires operational DEK).
    // Strictly kept as originally declared in v5.
    this.version(5).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, &sessionId, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      clinicalAddenda: '&id, sessionId, patientId, targetId, createdAt',
      appointments: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      waitingList: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt',
      invoices: '&id, patientId, episodeId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      payments: '&id, invoiceId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      expenses: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt',
      auditLogs: '&id, createdAt',
      staffState: '&id, updatedAt',
      episodeClinical: '&id, &episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      customWidgets: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt',
      migrationReports: '&id, createdAt',
      migrationState: '&id',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
      episodeSequences: '&episodeId',
    });

    // Version 6 Schema Definition (Phase 1A Follow-up: Index `patientId` on `waitingList`
    // and `patientId, episodeId` on `auditLogs` while keeping Version 5 immutable)
    this.version(6).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, &sessionId, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      clinicalAddenda: '&id, sessionId, patientId, targetId, createdAt',
      appointments: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      waitingList: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      invoices: '&id, patientId, episodeId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      payments: '&id, invoiceId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      expenses: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt',
      auditLogs: '&id, patientId, episodeId, createdAt',
      staffState: '&id, updatedAt',
      episodeClinical: '&id, &episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      customWidgets: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt',
      migrationReports: '&id, createdAt',
      migrationState: '&id',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
      episodeSequences: '&episodeId',
    });
  }

  /**
   * Soft-archive patient (Default safe clinical operation)
   */
  async archivePatient(patientId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.patients.update(patientId, {
      isArchived: 1,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  /**
   * Restore archived patient back to active list
   */
  async unarchivePatient(patientId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.patients.update(patientId, {
      isArchived: 0,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  /**
   * Audit dependent records for a patient across all v5 patient-linked tables before any potential hard deletion.
   */
  async auditPatientDependentRecords(patientId: string): Promise<PatientDependentRecordsSummary> {
    const episodes = await this.episodes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).toArray();
    const episodeIds = episodes.map((e) => e.id);
    const episodesCount = episodes.length;
    const sessionsCount = await this.sessions.where('patientId').equals(patientId).count();
    const soapNotesCount = await this.soapNotes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).count();
    const clinicalAddendaCount = await this.clinicalAddenda.where('patientId').equals(patientId).count();
    const appointmentsCount = await this.appointments.where('patientId').equals(patientId).count();
    const waitingListCount = await this.waitingList.where('patientId').equals(patientId).count();

    const patientInvoices = await this.invoices.where('patientId').equals(patientId).toArray();
    const episodeInvoices =
      episodeIds.length > 0
        ? await this.invoices.where('episodeId').anyOf(episodeIds).toArray()
        : [];
    const invoiceIdSet = new Set<string>([
      ...patientInvoices.map((i) => i.id),
      ...episodeInvoices.map((i) => i.id),
    ]);
    const invoicesCount = invoiceIdSet.size;

    const paymentsByPatient = await this.payments.where('patientId').equals(patientId).toArray();
    const paymentsByInvoice =
      invoiceIdSet.size > 0
        ? await this.payments.where('invoiceId').anyOf(Array.from(invoiceIdSet)).toArray()
        : [];
    const paymentIdSet = new Set<string>([
      ...paymentsByPatient.map((p) => p.id),
      ...paymentsByInvoice.map((p) => p.id),
    ]);
    const paymentsCount = paymentIdSet.size;

    const epClinicalByPatient = await this.episodeClinical.where('patientId').equals(patientId).toArray();
    const epClinicalByEpisode =
      episodeIds.length > 0
        ? await this.episodeClinical.where('episodeId').anyOf(episodeIds).toArray()
        : [];
    const epClinicalIdSet = new Set<string>([
      ...epClinicalByPatient.map((c) => c.id),
      ...epClinicalByEpisode.map((c) => c.id),
    ]);
    const episodeClinicalCount = epClinicalIdSet.size;

    return {
      patientId,
      episodesCount,
      sessionsCount,
      soapNotesCount,
      clinicalAddendaCount,
      appointmentsCount,
      waitingListCount,
      invoicesCount,
      paymentsCount,
      episodeClinicalCount,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Explicit permanent deletion with Patient Hard Deletion Safety Gate.
   * Strictly enforces:
   * 1. Audit of all dependent records across all v5 tables.
   * 2. Retention policy: Rejects deletion if clinical addenda exist.
   * 3. Requires valid and verified external backup proof; rejects deletion if missing, unverified, or corrupted.
   * 4. Atomically deletes all patient-linked records across all v5 tables (`patients`, `episodes`, `sessions`,
   *    `soapNotes`, `episodeSequences`, `appointments`, `waitingList`, `invoices`, `payments`, `episodeClinical`).
   *    Note: Clinic-wide non-patient tables (`expenses`, `customWidgets`, `staffState`, `auditLogs`) have no patient rows.
   */
  async deletePatientPermanently(
    patientId: string,
    backupProof?: { verifiedBackupString: string; bypassForTest?: boolean }
  ): Promise<void> {
    // 1. Audit dependent records
    const audit = await this.auditPatientDependentRecords(patientId);

    // 2. Retention policy rule: Cannot hard delete if clinical addenda exist
    if (audit.clinicalAddendaCount > 0) {
      throw new PatientHardDeletionBlockedError(
        `لا يمكن إجراء الحذف المادي النهائي للمريض: يحتوي الملف على (${audit.clinicalAddendaCount}) ملاحق سريرية رسمية (Clinical Addenda). تمنع سياسة الاحتفاظ السريري إتلاف هذه السجلات نهائياً.`
      );
    }

    // 3. Safety Gate: Require verified external backup proof
    if (!backupProof || !backupProof.verifiedBackupString) {
      throw new PatientHardDeletionBlockedError(
        'الحذف المادي النهائي للمريض معطل ومحظور (Safety Gate Active): يتطلب النظام وجود نسخة احتياطية خارجحة ناجحة ومتحقق من سلامتها قبل السماح بالإتلاف النهائي.'
      );
    }

    // 4. Validate backup proof integrity
    if (!backupProof.bypassForTest) {
      try {
        const raw = backupProof.verifiedBackupString;
        if (typeof raw !== 'string' || !raw.trim() || raw.length < 50) {
          throw new Error('حجم ملف النسخة صغير جداً.');
        }
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed.header?.magic !== 'PHYSIORECORD_BACKUP_V1.1') {
          throw new Error('المعرف السحري للنسخة الاحتياطية غير صالح.');
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ملف النسخة تالف أو غير صالح';
        throw new PatientHardDeletionBlockedError(
          `فشل التحقق من سلامة النسخة الاحتياطية قبل الحذف: ${msg}. تم حظر الحذف لحماية السجلات.`
        );
      }
    }

    // 5. Atomic deletion in transaction across all v5 patient-linked tables (no partial cascade)
    return this.transaction(
      'rw',
      [
        this.patients,
        this.episodes,
        this.sessions,
        this.soapNotes,
        this.clinicalAddenda,
        this.episodeSequences,
        this.appointments,
        this.waitingList,
        this.invoices,
        this.payments,
        this.episodeClinical,
      ],
      async () => {
        const inTxAddendaCount = await this.clinicalAddenda
          .where('patientId')
          .equals(patientId)
          .count();
        if (inTxAddendaCount > 0) {
          throw new PatientHardDeletionBlockedError(
            `لا يمكن إجراء الحذف المادي النهائي للمريض: يحتوي الملف على (${inTxAddendaCount}) ملاحق سريرية رسمية (Clinical Addenda).`
          );
        }

        const episodes = await this.episodes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).toArray();
        const episodeIds = episodes.map((e) => e.id);

        if (episodeIds.length > 0) {
          const episodeSessions = await this.sessions
            .where('episodeId')
            .anyOf(episodeIds)
            .toArray();
          const episodeSessionIds = episodeSessions.map((s) => s.id);
          if (episodeSessionIds.length > 0) {
            const sessionAddendaCount = await this.clinicalAddenda
              .where('sessionId')
              .anyOf(episodeSessionIds)
              .count();
            if (sessionAddendaCount > 0) {
              throw new PatientHardDeletionBlockedError(
                `لا يمكن إجراء الحذف المادي النهائي للمريض: توجد ملاحق سريرية مرتبطة بجلسات المريض.`
              );
            }
            await this.soapNotes.where('sessionId').anyOf(episodeSessionIds).delete();
          }
          await this.episodeSequences.where('episodeId').anyOf(episodeIds).delete();
          await this.episodeClinical.where('episodeId').anyOf(episodeIds).delete();
          await this.soapNotes.where('episodeId').anyOf(episodeIds).delete();
          await this.sessions.where('episodeId').anyOf(episodeIds).delete();
        }

        // Delete invoices & payments linked by patientId or episodeId
        const patientInvoices = await this.invoices.where('patientId').equals(patientId).toArray();
        const episodeInvoices =
          episodeIds.length > 0
            ? await this.invoices.where('episodeId').anyOf(episodeIds).toArray()
            : [];
        const invoiceIds = Array.from(
          new Set([...patientInvoices.map((i) => i.id), ...episodeInvoices.map((i) => i.id)])
        );
        if (invoiceIds.length > 0) {
          await this.payments.where('invoiceId').anyOf(invoiceIds).delete();
          await this.invoices.where('id').anyOf(invoiceIds).delete();
        }

        await this.payments.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
        await this.invoices.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
        await this.appointments.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
        await this.waitingList.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
        await this.episodeClinical.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
        await this.soapNotes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
        await this.sessions.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
        await this.episodes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
        await this.patients.delete(patientId);
      }
    );
  }

  /**
   * Cascade delete an episode and its child draft sessions, SOAP notes, episodeClinical, episodeSequences,
   * and episode-linked invoices/payments atomically.
   * Append-only integrity: clinical addenda are strictly non-deletable and block cascade deletion.
   */
  async deleteEpisodeCascade(episodeId: string): Promise<void> {
    return this.transaction(
      'rw',
      [
        this.episodes,
        this.sessions,
        this.soapNotes,
        this.clinicalAddenda,
        this.episodeSequences,
        this.episodeClinical,
        this.invoices,
        this.payments,
      ],
      async () => {
        // Find all session IDs under this episode
        const sessions = await this.sessions.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).toArray();
        const sessionIds = sessions.map((s) => s.id);

        if (sessionIds.length > 0) {
          // Append-only safeguard: clinical addenda must NEVER be cascade deleted
          const addendaCount = await this.clinicalAddenda
            .where('sessionId')
            .anyOf(sessionIds)
            .count();
          if (addendaCount > 0) {
            throw new Error(
              'فشل الحذف المتسلسل لحلقة الرعاية: توجد ملاحق سريرية (Clinical Addenda) مرتبطة بجلسات هذه الحلقة ولا يمكن حذفها.'
            );
          }

          // Delete child SOAP notes by sessionId
          await this.soapNotes.where('sessionId').anyOf(sessionIds).delete();
        }

        // Also delete any SOAP notes indexed by episodeId
        await this.soapNotes.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).delete();

        // Delete episode-scoped clinical store (ROM/MMT/bodyChart/HEP/snapshots)
        await this.episodeClinical.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).delete();
        await this.episodeClinical.delete(episodeId);

        // Delete episode-linked invoices and their child payments
        const episodeInvoices = await this.invoices.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).toArray();
        const invoiceIds = episodeInvoices.map((inv) => inv.id);
        if (invoiceIds.length > 0) {
          await this.payments.where('invoiceId').anyOf(invoiceIds).delete();
          await this.invoices.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).delete();
        }

        // Delete episode sequence record
        await this.episodeSequences.delete(episodeId);

        // Delete sessions
        await this.sessions.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).delete();

        // Delete episode
        await this.episodes.delete(episodeId);
      }
    );
  }

  /**
   * Cascade delete a single draft session and its child draft SOAP note atomically.
   * Append-only integrity: clinical addenda are strictly non-deletable and block cascade deletion.
   */
  async deleteSessionCascade(sessionId: string): Promise<void> {
    return this.transaction('rw', [this.sessions, this.soapNotes, this.clinicalAddenda], async () => {
      const addendaCount = await this.clinicalAddenda.where('sessionId').equals(sessionId).count();
      if (addendaCount > 0) {
        throw new Error(
          'فشل الحذف المتسلسل: لا يمكن حذف الجلسة لوجود ملاحق سريرية (Clinical Addenda) مسجلة لها. الملاحق سجلات غير قابلة للحذف.'
        );
      }
      await this.soapNotes.where('sessionId').equals(sessionId).delete();
      await this.sessions.delete(sessionId);
    });
  }
}

export const db = new PhysioRecordDatabase();

// Safely register test hooks strictly in test mode only (never expose db instance outside MODE === 'test')
if (import.meta.env.MODE === 'test') {
  registerTestHooks({
    db,
    PhysioRecordDatabase,
  });
}

/**
 * Ask browser to mark storage as persistent (prevents eviction under disk pressure)
 */
export async function requestPersistentStorage(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (navigator.storage && navigator.storage.persist) {
    try {
      const isPersisted = await navigator.storage.persist();
      console.log(`PhysioRecord storage persistence status: ${isPersisted ? 'Persistent' : 'Best-Effort'}`);
      return isPersisted;
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Storage persistence request failed:', e);
      return false;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Clinical Session Service Layer (v2.0)
// Centralized Envelope Encryption with Mandatory DEK Reservation & Referential Integrity
// =======================================================================

import { db as defaultDb, PhysioRecordDatabase } from '../db/db';
import {
  encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  computeSessionNumberHmacToken,
  getDekGeneration,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
} from '../crypto/vault';
import { isTestEnvironment, registerTestHooks } from '../utils/testHooks';
import { ReferentialIntegrityError } from '../db/registry';
import { EpisodeService } from './episodeService';
import { SoapService } from './soapService';
import {
  CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError,
  VaultGenerationChangedError,
  UnboundVaultKeyError,
  StaleTabOverwriteError,
  CURRENT_SOAP_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  CURRENT_SEAL_VERSION,
  type SealVerificationResult,
  type SessionRecord,
  type SessionClinicalData,
  type EncryptedSessionEntity,
  type SoapNoteData,
  type EncryptedSoapEntity,
  type SessionType,
  type SessionStatus,
  type AttendanceStatus,
} from '../types/clinical';
import { localDate } from '../utils/date';

export const HALF_FINALIZED_REPAIR_STATE_ID = 'half_finalized_soap_repair_v1';


interface LegacyEncryptedSessionEntity extends EncryptedSessionEntity {
  sessionDate?: string;
  sessionType?: SessionType;
  sessionStatus?: SessionStatus;
  status?: SessionStatus;
}

/**
 * Compute SHA-256 digest for content integrity verification
 */
async function computeSha256Digest(data: string): Promise<string> {
  const msgUint8 = new TextEncoder().encode(data);
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgUint8);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

function areUint8ArraysEqual(a: Uint8Array, b: Uint8Array): boolean {
  if (a.byteLength !== b.byteLength) return false;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.byteLength; i++) {
    if (a[i] !== b[i]) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

/**
 * Recursively canonicalizes an object with alphabetically sorted keys, omitting `undefined` values
 * and any keys listed in `excludeTopLevelKeys` at the root object level.
 */
export function canonicalizeObjectExcluding(
  value: unknown,
  excludeTopLevelKeys?: Set<string>
): unknown {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') {
    return value;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value.map((item) => canonicalizeObjectExcluding(item));
  }
  const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const sortedKeys = Object.keys(obj).sort();
  const result: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const key of sortedKeys) {
    if (excludeTopLevelKeys?.has(key)) continue;
    const v = obj[key];
    if (v === undefined) continue;
    result[key] = canonicalizeObjectExcluding(v);
  }
  return result;
}

/**
 * Deterministic canonical JSON serializer (sorted keys at every depth) for integrity seals.
 */
export function canonicalizeForSeal(value: unknown, excludeKeys?: string[]): string {
  return JSON.stringify(
    canonicalizeObjectExcluding(value, excludeKeys ? new Set(excludeKeys) : undefined)
  );
}

/**
 * Verifies the SHA-256 content integrity seal on a finalized SOAP note.
 * Returns a tri-state `SealVerificationResult` (`status: 'valid' | 'unsealed' | 'mismatch'`):
 * - `'unsealed'`: `contentDigestSha256` is absent (e.g., repaired legacy note) OR `sealVersion !== 1`
 *   (legacy note finalized by earlier code before canonical sorted-key seal v1).
 * - `'valid'`: `sealVersion === 1` and canonical SHA-256 digest matches `contentDigestSha256`.
 * - `'mismatch'`: `sealVersion === 1` and computed canonical SHA-256 digest differs from `contentDigestSha256`.
 */
export async function verifySoapSeal(
  soapData: SoapNoteData
): Promise<SealVerificationResult> {
  const expected = soapData.contentDigestSha256;
  if (!expected || soapData.sealVersion !== CURRENT_SEAL_VERSION) {
    return { status: 'unsealed', expected, actual: undefined };
  }
  const canonical = canonicalizeForSeal(soapData, ['contentDigestSha256']);
  const actual = await computeSha256Digest(canonical);
  return {
    status: actual === expected ? 'valid' : 'mismatch',
    expected,
    actual,
  };
}

/**
 * Verifies the SHA-256 content integrity seal on a finalized Session (and its linked SOAP note when present).
 * Returns a tri-state `SealVerificationResult` (`status: 'valid' | 'unsealed' | 'mismatch'`):
 * - `'unsealed'`: `contentIntegrityDigestSha256` is absent, `sessionData.sealVersion !== 1`, or the linked
 *   SOAP note is `'unsealed'`.
 * - `'mismatch'`: `sealVersion === 1` and either the SOAP seal is `'mismatch'`, an attended session is missing
 *   its SOAP note, or the session envelope digest does not match `contentIntegrityDigestSha256`.
 * - `'valid'`: both session and SOAP seals have `sealVersion === 1` and match their canonical SHA-256 digests.
 */
export async function verifySessionSeal(
  sessionData: SessionClinicalData,
  soapData?: SoapNoteData | null
): Promise<SealVerificationResult> {
  if (soapData) {
    const soapCheck = await verifySoapSeal(soapData);
    if (soapCheck.status === 'mismatch') {
      return {
        status: 'mismatch',
        expected: soapCheck.expected,
        actual: soapCheck.actual,
      };
    }
    if (soapCheck.status === 'unsealed') {
      return {
        status: 'unsealed',
        expected: soapCheck.expected,
        actual: soapCheck.actual,
      };
    }
  }

  const expected = sessionData.contentIntegrityDigestSha256;
  if (!expected || sessionData.sealVersion !== CURRENT_SEAL_VERSION) {
    return { status: 'unsealed', expected, actual: undefined };
  }

  if (!soapData && sessionData.attendanceStatus === 'attended') {
    return { status: 'mismatch', expected, actual: undefined };
  }

  const canonicalEnvelope = canonicalizeForSeal({
    session: canonicalizeObjectExcluding(
      sessionData,
      new Set(['contentIntegrityDigestSha256'])
    ),
    soapDigestSha256: soapData?.contentDigestSha256 ?? null,
  });
  const actual = await computeSha256Digest(canonicalEnvelope);
  return {
    status: actual === expected ? 'valid' : 'mismatch',
    expected,
    actual,
  };
}

/**
 * Keyed FIFO Async Mutex to guarantee atomic sequential execution per resource key (e.g. episodeId).
 * Solves concurrency race conditions without blocking concurrent operations on different keys.
 */
class AsyncKeyedMutex {
  private queues = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();

  async runExclusive<T>(key: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const currentQueue = this.queues.get(key) || Promise.resolve();

    let release: () => void;
    const nextInQueue = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      release = resolve;
    });

    const tail = currentQueue.then(() => nextInQueue, () => nextInQueue);
    this.queues.set(key, tail);

    await currentQueue;
    try {
      return await fn();
    } finally {
      if (this.queues.get(key) === tail) {
        this.queues.delete(key);
      }
      release!();
    }
  }
}

const sessionCreationMutex = new AsyncKeyedMutex();
